## Temporary follower NPC floating heads: stale armor reuse and load-buffer initialization (2026-09-27)

Scope: offline source/capture inspection and MSVC `/Zs` only. Read `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/COMMON.md`, `EVIDENCE.md` including FLOATING HEADS REPRODUCED, and the local evidence folder section 1. No game, full build, linking, deployment, or protocol executable was run. No edits to Harness*, ObjectService*, CreatorTogether or BoundPoseKeeper. This corrects a definite initialization defect; the changed build has **not** demonstrated an in-game fix.

### Capture proof: attachment succeeds, then an obsolete clone is reused

Evidence is `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/captures/heads-1105/follower_tp_client.log`, `actor_FF000DB3.json`, `actor_FF000DB5.json`, and `detached.json`. ArmorAttachmentTrace is logged as `ArmorTrace`. Its sequence/QPC event fields identify the native operation; the log-prefix thread/time belong to the later drain and must not be treated as execution timing.

| Actor | Initial attachment | Root replacement | Reuse under the obsolete parent |
|---|---|---|---|
| Headsman FF000DB3, biped `988F6150`, owner handle `10FC28` | Line 7220, seq289, AttachArmor: destination `189828560`, result `1898C4CB8`, resultParent `189828560`. Initial SetRoot is seq286, line 7193. | Lines 7397-7407, seq316: input outer root `1898F7D90`; biped skeleton changes `1898265A0` -> `189826168`. Slot 2 remains clone `1898C4CB8`, parent `189828560`. | Lines 7411/7416, seq317: bufferedClone `1898C4CB8`, bufferedParent `189828560` becomes current clone with that same parent. Seq318 repeats it. |
| Priestess FF000DB5, biped `9930CA80`, owner handle `10FC2A` | Line 7362, seq308, AttachArmor: destination `189828000`, result `1898F5A98`, resultParent `189828000`. Initial SetRoot is seq305, line 7335. | Lines 7567-7577, seq337: input outer root `1898F76D8`; biped skeleton changes `1898262D0` -> `189909AB8`. Slot 2 remains clone `1898F5A98`, parent `189828000`. | Lines 7581/7586, seq338: bufferedClone `1898F5A98`, bufferedParent `189828000` becomes current clone with that same parent. Seq339 repeats it. |

Both final actor captures report the new biped root in the actor tree, but the identical old slot-2 clone outside it (`cloneInActorTree:false`, `skinned:true`). Thus this run is **not** a null-destination AttachArmor failure. The exact demonstrated bad operation is SetRoot replacing the biped skeleton while populated parts survive, followed by LoadParts accepting their old nonnull parents as reusable. No detach is required to produce this condition. Worn snapshots at lines 9213-9214 match the owner and cannot detect this ancestry failure. `detached.json` also lists the local player; that separate first/third-person diagnostic case is not attributed to this NPC defect.

The initial premise needs a correction: lines 6568/6574 (`Spawn Actor`) come from `HookSpawnActorInWorld`, an UpdateReference3D request hook, not specifically Actor::Create. Lines 6853-6855 and 6886-6888 show OnAssignCharacter adopting native FF000DB3/DB5 and retiring the earlier server-spawned proxies FF0008FB/8FA. The requested two actors therefore need proof at native remote adoption as well as the custom WaitingFor3D path.

### Spawn-path audit and source defect

OnCharacterSpawn and CreateCharacterForEntity resolve a supplied static base or leveled pick and call `TESNPC::Deserialize` on it before Actor::Create. With no supplied stable base, TESNPC::Create allocates a private base, initializes defaults, and deserializes it. Actor::Create enters native reference spawning (13723); root construction/armor completion can be asynchronous. FaceGenSystem changes existing head materials/tints, not body parentage. WaitingFor3D applies inventory after the first available root; SetActorInventory can queue a parts reset, but this capture has no SetActorInventory entry for DB3/DB5. Native adoption reconciles state and moves the reference. Pose copy writes pose/transforms and does not attach the absent armor clone. None of these distinctions licenses an unconditional Reset3D or manual AttachChild repair.

The definite producer defect is in `BGSLoadFormBuffer::BGSLoadFormBuffer`: it calls the **base** BGSLoadGameBuffer constructor but omitted construction of the BGSLoadFormData subobject at +0x28. In particular, byte +0x48 was indeterminate. Native TESNPC::LoadGame treats bit 3 there as `kConstructedForm` and initializes its reset mask to `0x3B`. When Deserialize targets a shared NPC base, the native Reset3DMobIterator marks **every existing actor with that base** for that update. The `0x20` bit requests a complete Load3D; `0x01` also requests worn reconstruction. This explains how a no-op appearance load can cause the second root and two LoadParts operations without our calling Reset3D for the affected actor.

**Attribution limit:** the capture proves the second-root/stale-clone transition. It does not record the appearance ChangeFlags, buffer +0x48, reset mask, or Reset3DMobIterator invocation. The uninitialized-field trigger is proven in source and fits the event ordering, but its exact value in this run is not measured. Race/sex changes can legitimately select the same reset mask. Do not report complete upstream causal proof or a validated floating-head fix until changed-build observations establish it.

| Address Library ID / function VA | Inspected behavior and use |
|---|---|
| 35993 / `0x140637820` | BGSLoadGameBuffer constructor initializes only the base region through +0x20; it cannot initialize form flags +0x48. |
| 35984 / `0x140637370` | BGSLoadFormData constructor initializes metadata, including flags and padding at subobject +0x20..+0x22. Adopt this native constructor at buffer +0x28 before setting caller fields. Native function `0x140638130` demonstrates the two-constructor sequence. |
| 24778 / `0x1403C7B10` | TESNPC::LoadGame reads constructed-form bit +0x48:3, race and sex changes; dispatches reset mask. |
| 24390 / `0x1403B06B0`, 24393 / `0x1403B0790`, 39907 / `0x140724600` | Reset3DMobIterator traverses processes, tests actor base-pointer equality, ORs requested flags into AIProcess middle data +0x311. |
| 39395 / `0x1406F6720` | Update3DModel_Impl flag 0x20 removes old 3D and invokes Load3D; flag 1 separately rebuilds worn parts. |
| 37177 / `0x1406713E0`, 19727 / `0x1402E9EE0`, 24721 / `0x1403C26C0` | Actor/reference/NPC Load3D chain; NPC model creation reuses the actor's existing BipedAnim. |
| 15657 / `0x140217D90` | SetRoot selects the new NPC Root and writes biped+8; does not rebind retained clones. Trace caller `0x1403C284F` is inside 24721. |
| 15678 / `0x140218E50` | LoadParts reuse checks item/model equality, nonnull clone and immediate parent, not ancestry under the new actor root. |
| 15711 / `0x14021CE30`, 15712 / `0x14021D170` | Skinned dispatcher and AttachArmor bind skins and attach to destination; seq289/308 prove this first attachment succeeded. Manual reparenting is rejected because bone references also belong to the old skeleton. |
| 40255 / `0x140739A40`, 15659 / `0x140217E30`, 15661 / `0x140218000` | Reset/removal machinery inspected; no unconditional rebuild or global root hook added. |
| 40263 / `0x14073A760` | Returns address of actor's biped member at +0x268. Proof reads that member, then slot-2 clone at biped+0x120 and NiAVObject parent+0x30. |
| 56626 / `0x140A5D370`, 37313 / `0x140677370`, 19799 / `0x1402F1E50` | MoveTo actor branch is StopInteractingQuick -> ForceState -> cell/position reconciliation. The direct disable/enable branch is for non-actors. Do not substitute the existing simplified MoveTo comment for causal evidence. |

### References and adopted/rejected approaches

- [CommonLibSSE-NG BGSLoadFormData](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/B/BGSLoadFormData.h), [BGSLoadFormBuffer](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/B/BGSLoadFormBuffer.h), [BipedAnim](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/B/BipedAnim.h): inspected local source under `C:/Tools/ref/CommonLibSSE-NG`. Adopt the subobject boundary, constructed-form flag meaning and biped layout, verified against the exact executable. Use native field initialization rather than an offset guess or clearing rebuild flags after the fact.
- [TiltedEvolution-rwf CharacterService](https://github.com/rfortier/TiltedEvolution-rwf/blob/features-integration/Code/client/Services/Generic/CharacterService.cpp), local revision `a7d061564338ab7ff475d6919a19c42664589611`: both temporary-spawn entry points likewise Deserialize the resolved base before Actor::Create. Useful origin/path comparison, not evidence that the inherited partial load-buffer construction is correct.
- [SKSE64 PapyrusActorBase](https://github.com/ianpatt/skse64/blob/master/skse64/PapyrusActorBase.cpp): inspected original source for weight, height and face data setters. These mutate NPC data and are not an armor-parent repair; reject applying arbitrary appearance/weight changes to fix the symptom.
- Considered private NPC CopyFrom (24664 / `0x1403BDD00`, appearance copy 24665 / `0x1403BDFC0`). It deep-copies face storage but installs `faceNPC=source`; clearing that link changes body-weight lookup and GetTemplateBase identity. Rejected this broader redesign for this patch: constructing the omitted native metadata fixes the established defect while retaining legitimate appearance semantics.

### Changes, review and validation

- `Code/client/Games/SaveLoad.cpp`, BGSLoadFormBuffer constructor: call native 35984 on `&formId` (+0x28), with offset assertions, before installing supplied change flags/version. All network NPC load buffers receive initialized metadata; no actor/form special cases.
- `Code/client/Games/Forms.cpp`, TESNPC::Deserialize: empty appearance with zero change flags is a no-op; empty data with nonzero flags is rejected. Nonempty body trailers still take the existing path. This also prevents unnecessary shared-base LoadGame when the sender sent no appearance.
- `Code/client/Services/Generic/NpcLootService.cpp` and `.h`: after the existing three-second settled worn observation, emit once per temporary actor form in that observation state: `Spawned copy {:X}: body attached {}`. Read slot-2 clone ancestry, bounded to 64 parents, and recheck actor root, biped and slot clone identity. Covers native DB3/DB5 replacement and custom copies. No scene mutation or new scan; diagnostic state is cleared with the existing session/state lifetime.
- Independent Reviewer A and Reviewer B inspected source, capture, and final patch. Both approved the native constructor and empty-payload fixes with no blocking finding. B initially preferred private bases; after inspecting template identity/appearance implications, both agreed the narrower initialization correction is appropriate. Agreement is review, not runtime validation. Both require the upstream attribution limit above.
- MSVC `/Zs` passed for SaveLoad.cpp, Forms.cpp, and NpcLootService.cpp using the existing client dependency manifest flags, with forced TiltedOnlinePCH.h and no PCH artifact/object/link output. The helper emitted a nonfatal `vswhere.exe` PATH warning before cl.exe successfully checked all three files. No runtime protocol tests or in-game tests were executed, as explicitly required by the task.

Next permitted paired run: look for `Spawned copy FF000DB3: body attached true` and the equivalent DB5 line, then confirm actual visible body geometry and matching host/follower captures. The log is a read-only ancestry observation, **not** proof of skin/render visibility or a loader completion barrier; false also covers unreadable/missing slot 2 (including legitimate slotless creatures), excessive ancestry depth, or changing identity. It remains non-atomic despite identity rechecks. If the same second-root sequence remains, inspect the recorded producer flags/call path rather than repeating timed rebuilds. Preserve corpse/dismemberment, player appearance, inventory/loot, and native quest-actor identity behavior. Exercise other temporary NPCs, nonempty appearance/body data, late join/re-entry and ownership changes. Cost added by the fix is O(1) native metadata initialization per appearance load; the diagnostic adds at most 64 parent reads once per observed temporary form, O(A) over A such forms per receiving client, independent of bodies B, with O(P*A) aggregate copy observations across P clients. No new per-frame actor scan, transport traffic, or five-player performance claim.


## 2026-09-27: bound pose restoration in ordinary creator play

Scope: `BoundPoseKeeper.cpp/.h`, `CreatorTogether.cpp`, and this append-only research entry. Read `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/COMMON.md`, `EVIDENCE.md`, and `captures/harness-20260927-103856/ANALYSIS.md` section 3. No game, xmake, object/link build, deployment or commit. COMMON explicitly exempts this worker from Reviewer A/B; neither reviewed this patch. Existing concurrent changes are preserved. Harness*, ObjectService*, SaveLoad.cpp, Forms.cpp and NpcLootService were not edited.

### Evidence and diagnosis

The capture shows local hand separation increasing from 2.45 to 57.31/57.81 on the host and 46.43 to 56.57/58.17 on the follower. The host's follower replica stays around 45-47, while the follower's host replica stays around 2.46-2.47 (HJ:68135,68857,69560,68369,69091,69794; FJ:67871,68589,69292,68121,68824,69527). No successful repair was logged. Section 3 explicitly distinguishes diagnostics from repair: `IsRenderDiagnosticsArmed()` explained missing traces, but the prior repair already ran outside that switch. The capture cannot identify which silent guard rejected it.

Source defects addressed: restoration required a preexisting bound pose, even when sampled after the native race/sex rebuild; preview-only observation missed the shared preset rebuild; normal Done without a race/sex change had no completion replay after menu teardown. On replicas, an already-unbound copy could never pass that pose predicate, and a queued Reset3D discarded the immediate repair without completion work. These mechanisms are source-supported; their individual contributions to the recorded failure are not measured.

### Primary references inspected before editing

- Web search located [CommonLibSSE-NG RaceSexMenu source](https://ng.commonlib.dev/RaceSexMenu_8h_source.html). Also read the actual [alandtse header](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/a898f469851c464d05137bb74b069dd234897643/include/RE/R/RaceSexMenu.h) in `C:/Tools/ref/CommonLibSSE-NG`: adopt menu callback/lifecycle structure; reject treating unnamed rebuild bytes as a bound-state predicate or copying another runtime's offsets.
- Read actual [TiltedEvolution-rwf Games/Animation.cpp](https://github.com/rfortier/TiltedEvolution-rwf/blob/a7d061564338ab7ff475d6919a19c42664589611/Code/client/Games/Animation.cpp) locally, then this tree's Games/Animation.cpp and AnimationSystem.cpp. Adopt the existing hooked local action entry, variable/flag capture and ordinary peer queue. Reject direct local holder-only send (no publication), a new protocol and extra ForceAction bursts. Native creator repair has no idle-form ID; the existing event-name action path carries it.
- Fresh offline native reads: N/AL52346, VA **0x140968830**, rebuilds through AL52391, VA **0x14096BCF0**, then sends `OffsetBoundStandingPlayerInstant` and clears +1A3/+1A4. It does not require a preexisting bound clip. AL52345, VA **0x140968010**, and preset caller VA **0x14096AE30** also use 52391 without that send. Adopt this shared rebuild boundary. Offline PE disassembly verified RCX carries the live menu at 0x1409686FA, 0x140968974 and 0x14096AEE6 before calls to 52391; the detour forwards once with `void(void*)`.
- AL52343, VA **0x140967B40** (also deleting destructor **0x140972F20**), restores creator graph variables. Adopt a single main-frame completion after the menu becomes closed; this does not assert that the destructor alone produced the observed hand separation.
- AL40255, VA **0x140739A40**, removes biped parts before its queue decision. AL39033, VA **0x1406E49E0**, decides whether to queue; AL39395, VA **0x1406F6720**, consumes model flags and rebuilds; AL39911, VA **0x140724680**, reads those flags. Adopt deferring our appearance mutation in its existing coalesced mailbox until the native synchronous path and graph are ready. Reject destroying parts first and abandoning an immediate repair when the task is merely queued.
- AL38949, VA **0x1406DFBC0**, with precomputed flag 2 reaches AL43567, VA **0x1407D7AC0**, which sends the holder event. AL38953, VA **0x1406DFF90**, is the existing peer execution path. Retain both transport publication and the one-action peer budget unchanged.

### Changes and bounds

`BoundPoseKeeper` now distinguishes explicit native creator restoration from preservation of a sampled pose during other appearance changes. A 52391 detour restores preview/preset rebuilds during shared New Game creation; other previews preserve only their actual pre-rebuild pose. The existing 52346 completion hook publishes race/sex restoration without querying the already-rebuilt pose, following that native path's own unconditional send. Shared creator closure restores once on the first safe main frame, independent of diagnostics, with a two-second readiness deadline and cancellation on a changed cell, missing player, nonzero life/knock state or service destruction. Reopening the menu cancels the prior completion. Participation is retained only across this menu's open/close edge, so a later ordinary showracemenu close cannot manufacture bound intent. This is a short creator-completion operation, not continuous bound-pose enforcement or a saved offset state. Ordinary appearance changes still require the observed bound clip.

`CreatorTogether` defers replica appearance mutations before a potentially queued Reset3D, applies native restoration for live/final creator looks while participating in shared New Game creation, and retains the existing two-rebuild frame budget. Late final looks outside that window preserve observed bound state; the owner's accepted completion also arrives through normal animation sync. It consumes `InCreator` from an identical-cosmetics final packet; previously the equality shortcut left the old metadata unchanged. This newly relevant lifecycle defect could otherwise classify a later normal appearance change as a creator change. Local repairs use the existing action hook for peer delivery; separate replica rebuild repairs send only to that replica's holder.

Read-only traces remain under `IsRenderDiagnosticsArmed()` and their existing bounded buffers/budgets. The flag is now named `s_traceEnabled`, and its arm log says `readOnly=true`. The success line retains the required prefix and adds actor/local identity. Disarmed traces cannot suppress gameplay restoration.

Added idle-frame work is O(1): a menu-state edge check, plus one readiness check per frame only during a pending local completion (at most two seconds). No new actor/body scan or per-frame allocation was added. Existing queued-appearance work is O(P); expensive rebuild/pose preservation stays capped at two per frame, with the existing pose walk bounded at 512 nodes. Native creator work is O(R) for R actual menu rebuild callbacks, independent of A actors/B bodies. Existing network action fanout applies, with no new packet format. These are code bounds, not a measured five-player performance result.

### Validation and next-run acceptance

Final `BoundPoseKeeper.cpp` and `CreatorTogether.cpp` both passed MSVC `/Zs` (exit 0), using existing dependency-manifest flags, forced `TiltedOnlinePCH.h`, and only the helper definitions before `objects = []` in `Code/tests/run_world_state_checks.py`. The MSVC environment setup printed a preexisting `vswhere.exe` warning; cl executed both checks successfully. `git diff --check` also passed for the edited tracked files. No protocol executable or game was run under this task's syntax-only restriction.

The next coordinator run must use the changed build on BOTH PCs with render diagnostics unarmed. Require `Bound pose: re-applied after rebuild (creator closed) actor=14 local=true` in EACH client log, plus native creator/replica appearance success when those rebuilds occur. A success line proves accepted dispatch only. In existing harness pose captures, require close-hand geometry after creator teardown for both local 14s and both peer representations, sustained through later post-close samples (the prior close-hand reference was approximately 2.5 units; prior failures were 45-58). Do not score a null `variable01`, an old build's log, one transient close sample, or accepted dispatch alone as a pose pass.

Exercise Done without race/sex edits, preset/race/sex edits, viewing the other character, identical final cosmetics and delayed peer appearances. Require no incomplete/rejected/timed-out repair warning; a warning or a pose mismatch is a FAIL requiring new evidence. After native cutting/freeing, ordinary subsequent appearance changes must remain unbound and no creator completion may keep enforcing the pose. Check late final-look ordering and native readiness in the paired run. Modded creator/behavior graphs and same-cell load interruption during the short completion window remain runtime compatibility cases. Five-player correctness/costs remain unvalidated. This is an implemented candidate with syntax evidence, **not an observed in-game fix**.

## 2026-09-27: missing remote NPC worn stock and death hold (partial implementation, not in-game validated)

Request: read COMMON.md, EVIDENCE.md, harness-20260927-103856/ANALYSIS.md section 5, and the approved `agent/archive-round6/task_inv.md`; no game, xmake build, deployment, or commit. Only `NakedNpcGuard.cpp/.h` and this research record were edited for this task. All named protected files, including NpcLootService.cpp, were left alone.

### Evidence and causal boundary

Follower `tp_client.log:19471,19582,19662,23613,23631,23644` records the Ralof owner armor snapshot versus local empty worn set and `unavailable 2`. Those observations distinguish missing usable candidates from a detached populated body part, but do not identify the earlier inventory writer. The old `UpdateNative` could only equip positive existing candidates and never supplied missing stock; a complete sequenced snapshot was observational rather than a complete appearance application. Retrying that branch cannot recover zero stock. Dynamic enchant identity was also compared using process-local IDs, unlike the existing NpcSameItem wire identity helper.

Follower `:16943` already has Lokir life 2/local empty, `:16951` holds clothing changes while pending=true, `:16961` receives the latest owner clothing, `:16996` remains empty, and `:17002` finally releases the hold. The 1,750 ms quiet/root settle rules and death-pending gate explicitly held both clothing replay and repair. This explains the demonstrated delayed recovery; it does not prove which native/script operation initially removed the clothes, or guarantee zero visible frames after the patch.

### Primary source research (web search and actual source inspection)

- [CommonLibSSE-NG ActorEquipManager.cpp](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/src/RE/A/ActorEquipManager.cpp), inspected in the local read-only reference checkout. Adopted explicit extra-list equip/unequip, queue=false and applyNow=true; the API returns no equip success result, so proof must reread worn flags. No new detour or offset.
- [rfortier/TiltedEvolution-rwf InventoryService.cpp](https://github.com/rfortier/TiltedEvolution-rwf/blob/master/Code/client/Services/Generic/InventoryService.cpp), local source around 296-367 inspected. Adopted retaining unchanged equipment and stock. Rejected its owner-only/dead exclusion for a remote corpse appearance apply; default outfit restoration cannot express the owner's current worn set.
- [MrMartexX/TiltedEvolution PR 10](https://github.com/MrMartexX/TiltedEvolution/pull/10) and local InventoryService source inspected. Rejected ResetInventory/RemoveAllItems as an appearance repair: neither an NPC base outfit nor local availability establishes owner intent, and blanket resets detach working equipment.
- Approved task_inv design inspected alongside `archive-round6/report_inv_r1.md`. The latter explicitly rolled back physical render-copy creation and the unfinished loot adapter. Current server `NpcInventoryRelay::Loot` still denies requests; client NpcLootService is an observer/publisher. Consequently this change **does not claim to implement the full approved invisible-render-copy/lazy-loot design**.

### Changes and explicit remaining work

- Main-thread armor stock/equipment replay is separated from the retained non-armor death hold. Known appearance is applied as soon as root/process are ready, even when life is 1/2 or death is pending; life/root changes invalidate the check deadline. Existing bounded eight-actor visits and one-second stable repair deadline remain.
- Sequenced worn snapshots supersede form-only equipment diffs. Negative contents deltas cannot erase sequenced worn intent (removing a spare must not undress the remaining worn item). Snapshot cleanup unequips only items absent from a completely mapped owner set after desired items are available; it never clears the inventory.
- Missing stock can be reconstructed **only when a full assignment/spawn contents baseline independently confirms the owner owns that instance**. Add one unworn item under ScopedInventoryOverride, reread stock, reacquire its extra list, then silently equip it; leave already-correct instances untouched. Appearance comparison uses NpcSameItem (dynamic enchant identity and intentionally ignored tempering). No raw native extra-list pointer is retained across frames.
- Ordered stock deltas record their owner post-delta counts. A delayed add is capped by that count minus actual local count, preventing duplication while preserving a genuine second owner item. Four compile-time regression assertions cover delayed delivery, new spare, partial delivery and missing stock.
- A failure latch is recorded only when an added item cannot be identified by the native reread; successful supply is not a historical latch that could block later repair. Unknown form mapping prevents empty-slot cleanup. Missing quest-alias items, negative/inconsistent counts, and unknown owner contents defer instead of creating untracked lootable render copies.

**Remaining blocker to the requested universal design:** a worn snapshot alone (including after ownership transfer) has no authoritative contents baseline; render copies cannot safely be hidden/excluded while the approved lazy-loot adapter is disabled. These states intentionally emit `Worn stock deferred` and remain unrepaired. This is a guarded stock-reconstruction/appearance patch, not the requested complete migration. Ordinary content deltas/full-assignment paths remain outside the changed guard, and no native inventory counting/menu adapter was introduced. Do not describe this as a universal fix or assume the captured Ralof instance has a usable contents baseline without the next-run proof.

### Validation, review, and next-run proof

MSVC `/Zs` passed for NakedNpcGuard.cpp and its changed header, including four static_assert delivery cases. The compiler environment emits a non-fatal `vswhere.exe` PATH warning; cl.exe completed with exit code 0. No executable protocol tests, linking, game launch, deployment, or paired observations were performed.

COMMON.md waives unavailable external review. Two independent peer reviews were performed; these are not claimed as those external reviewers. Both caught the spare-removal/sequence regression, historical supply latch and unknown-baseline duplication risk. Those were addressed by retaining sequence authority, latching only failed native identification and requiring confirmed owner contents before synthesis. Both also identified the unfinished lazy-loot/render-copy exclusion, which remains explicitly unresolved rather than being called validated.

Next paired run (coordinator only):

1. `Worn stock: <actor> server <id> epoch <epoch> seq <seq> item <mod:form> total 0 -> 1 candidate true` proves missing stock was supplied. `Worn stock deferred: ... stock-known false` or `owner-stock 0` identifies the remaining baseline gap; it is a failure, not a repair pass.
2. `Worn set: ... equipped ... supplied ... matches true stock-known ... before [...] after [...] life ... pending ... held ...` proves the armor set at that main-thread observation. A second observation must show supplied=0/equipped=0 for unchanged intent. A nonzero held count may belong to non-armor data and must not delay clothing.
3. For death, correlate owner sequence, local worn set, life/pending and existing `Worn mesh`/ArmorTrace/harness biped records. Check from the first death transition, not merely three seconds after receipt. Worn flags alone are not mesh attachment or absence-of-visible-naked-frames proof.
4. `Worn stock delivery: ... delta 1 applied 0 owner-count 1 local-count 1` demonstrates delayed delivery deduplication; a genuinely new second item must apply one at owner-count 2/local-count 1. Also check spare removal, fully empty owner set, loading, ownership transfer, enchanted/quest items and loot before considering completion.

Cost: existing target metadata O(A) per 250 ms and at most eight native actor visits/frame remain. Stable scans are O(I) per due actor for its armor entries; reconciliation is O(W*I) for W owner worn items and I local armor entries. New baseline delta bookkeeping is O(I) per queued inventory event, plus native stock reads for armor additions. No new network messages or player/body scans; peer fan-out is unchanged. No measured five-player performance or paired gameplay correctness claim.

New proof uses owned actor lookup, balanced by DecRefHandle, and retains no pointer across callbacks. |

### Chosen fix, references, rejected alternatives

- New `Games/Skyrim/BipedArmorRepair.cpp/.h`: immediately before native LoadParts, inspect all 42 buffered slots. When a cached ARMO has matching requested item/model, a nonnull clone/parent, and bounded ancestry definitively outside the current actor skeleton, evict that buffered entry with native 15661(clear=true). Leave current descriptors intact. Original LoadParts then reconstructs and rebinds through the native attachment path. This covers rigid and skinned armor, all forms, native adoption and synthetic spawn, without remote/temporary gates on the repair. Healthy same-tree cache reuse and mismatched entries retain native behavior. Missing tree, biped/tree disagreement, or ancestry beyond 64 links is unknown and is not mutated.
- `ArmorAttachmentTrace.cpp`: share its existing LoadParts/CompletePart detours with the production helpers. Install those two hooks regardless of diagnostic enablement; `Begin` returns immediately when trace recording is off. Other trace hooks/records remain opt-in and read-only. `FunctionHookManager` uses MinHook, whose duplicate target creation does not chain callbacks; two independent hooks would risk silently losing either fix or trace. No hook-library changes.
- Proof `Spawned copy <id>: body attached <bool> phase=LoadParts/CompletePart ancestry=<0 attached,1 detached,2 unknown> rebuiltSlots=<hex>` executes at native load and asynchronous body completion, independent of worn snapshots, inventory differences, remote assignment or trace enablement. `rebuiltSlots` records cache eviction requests, not guaranteed successful asset loading. The proof checks both biped-root and body-clone ancestry against the loader's current tree. It is not a rendering/skin-visibility or main-thread publication guarantee; async loads can first report false and later complete. Slotless creatures can legitimately report false.
- Searched open-source Skyrim/CommonLib armor-root work before patching. Inspected [CommonLibSSE-NG BipedAnim layout](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/B/BipedAnim.h) and [native RemoveAllParts wrapper](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/src/RE/B/BipedAnim.cpp) locally under `C:/Tools/ref/CommonLibSSE-NG`. Adopted its current/buffered layout and native ownership conventions, verified against this executable. Rejected calling RemoveAllParts at SetRoot: it clears requested descriptors and expands mutation to unrelated weapon state before normal loader preparation.
- Rejected manual AttachChild, parent-pointer clearing, inventory re-equipping, delayed full Reset3D, and per-frame actor repair scans. Native skin attachment must also rebind bone/matrix references; a parent-only move is insufficient. The chosen cache boundary already has prepared worn state and native scheduling. Preserved the earlier SaveLoad/Forms initialization fix, but did not rely on it to prevent every root replacement.

### headsfix2 round 1: response to headsfix2-verify0 (2026-09-27)

Read the complete `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/conductor/headsfix2-verify0.log`. The diagnosis stands with high confidence; the review correctly identified an added raw-pointer observation hazard. This revision hardens that path and supplies executed offline evidence for native descriptor preservation/cache dispatch. It does **not** establish in-game rendering success or race freedom. The original task explicitly prohibits launching the game.

Reviewer-point disposition:

- **Always-on crash surface: accepted and hardened.** `BipedArmorRepair.cpp` now bulk-copies the biped through the tracer's `ReadProcessMemory` pattern, guards item-type and every parent read, and treats failed/partial reads and depth exhaustion as Unknown. Before native eviction it rereads the owner/root, TLS-aware actor tree, both complete slot records and clone parent, and repeats the ancestry predicates. Observed changes skip mutation. Proof uses guarded snapshots and reports `nonAtomic=1`; an observed root/clone change reports Unknown. Held actor references still protect actor method calls. These reads do not acquire an engine lock, prevent ABA, or close the final check-to-native-call window. Native mutation continues on the original loader thread under its existing lifetime/scheduling contract. This is an added production path versus `25fa030d`, with remaining scheduling, resource-load and performance risks; preserving earlier behavior is not proof of no regression.
- **15661 scope and ABI: verified against instructions and exercised offline.** AL15661 / `0x140218000` takes RCX=biped, RDX=part, R8B=clear, R9D=model replacement. It saves RDX in RDI at `0x140218033`; clone clear is `[RDI+0x20]` at `0x140218272`; clear=true writes item/addon/model at `[RDI]`, `[RDI+8]`, `[RDI+0x10]` at `0x14021837B/37E/384`, then releases that part's handle arena. The native loader AL15678 / `0x140218E50` itself calls this same function with the **buffered** slot, clear=1, replacement=0 at `0x1402190BC` when rejecting a cached clone. Current and Buffered are disjoint arrays (`+0x10`, `+0x13C0`, stride `0x78`, 42 entries). This is the engine's own cache-rejection operation, not an invented teardown ABI. ARMO (type 0x1A) takes the single-clone detach branch; the broader weapon-parent/sibling branch requires type 0x29 and is excluded by our guard.
- **Rebuild is a dispatch guarantee, not a resource-success guarantee.** The fast path at `0x140218FB1..0x140219026` checks requested model, buffered clone and its nonzero parent, and item/model equality. Clearing the buffered item/model/clone prevents that reuse. AL15651 / `0x1402178D0` then resets the buffered slot, including remaining `partHandle +0x70`; Current remains the requested input. AL15678 reaches native resource loading and AL15711 / `0x14021CE30` on synchronous success, or native async submission and AL15701 / `0x14021BFA0` on completion. AL15712 / `0x14021D170` resolves bones against biped+8 and rewrites bone/world-transform pointers before attaching geometry. Missing assets or unsuccessful native attachment can still fail. Withdraw any reading of the earlier wording as guaranteed correct visible re-skinning.
- **Deferred detach target: source-confirmed.** AL36900 / `0x140667B50` increments the supplied old clone's reference count, copies that pointer and owner handle into task 0x38, and enqueues it. AL15660 / `0x140217F70` detaches the supplied clone. Thus the task retains the old object; the queue does not retarget a subsequently built clone. AL15665 / `0x140218560` cleans clone-related extra data; it does not clear Current descriptors.
- **Incomplete short sequence table: corrected in the rewritten task report.** Full sequence is DB3 SetRoot 323 / LoadParts 324 / AttachArmor 326 / SetRoot 335 / LoadParts 336,337; DB5 338 / 339 / 341 / 344 / 345,346. Detailed original table above was already correct. `trace-r1-sequence.json` and `trace-r1-evidence.txt` preserve line numbers, native TIDs and QPC intervals. For these recorded calls, the second root follows completion of the first load; different threads alone do not demonstrate simultaneous mutation of the same biped. This does not rule out unrecorded concurrent detach or other runs.
- **Previously unverifiable checks: made reproducible.** Saved follower executable rehashed to `2cfaf2c0741d879a685336fb2f449d5dc67bfcf49ea52f82dd0db3d160612a85`; `Spawned copy`, `body attached`, and `Biped armor repair enabled` remain absent; `Worn evidence: settled` remains at byte 931600. The historical 10:58-build/11:15-source comparison remains the earlier audit, not a fresh current-build timestamp assertion. All cited AL mappings were queried again and native text saved as `headsfix2-check/native-<id>.txt`. CommonLib layouts were reopened locally and online. Fresh MSVC checks and standalone regression logs are UTF-8. No separate reviewer approvals are claimed for this revision; COMMON.md waives that requirement, and the earlier claimed approvals are not used as evidence.
- **Live rebuild and second-root trigger: not claimed.** A new-binary paired run is prohibited in this task and still required before saying the visual issue is fixed. The saved trace settles the invalid-cache mechanism but lacks the upstream stack/reset flags selecting the second root. Preventing that specific request is not required for the general cache repair; no further live instrumentation was added to chase it.

Executed validation artifacts, all under the local evidence folder:

1. `repair-guard-test.cpp/.cmd`, `repair-guard-test-rerun.log`: compiles the actual production helper with test doubles for engine lookup/GetNiNode/RemovePart, and uses real Windows `ReadProcessMemory`. Passed all 42 slot positions (including rigid slots), Current preservation, healthy/mismatched/unknown cases, cyclic ancestry, PAGE_NOACCESS biped/item/clone/parent reads, changed tree/slot/parent revalidation, proof on unreadable geometry, and balanced owner references. This validates helper decisions and guarded reads, not the mocked native teardown.

## Corpse dynamic steering and independent pose evidence (2026-09-27)

Request: keep follower copies dynamic, stream the complete owner ragdoll during the fall, preserve native constraints and door-crash auditing, and capture independent per-body and rendered-bone errors. Resolved reviewer bounds disagreement against executable before another measurement: loader AL11595 / VA0x14015B040 reads XPRM 0x20 bytes and passes the raw vector to AL15518 / VA0x140212710, which forwards it unchanged to BGSPrimitiveBox size setter AL15533 / VA0x1402132F0. Point containment AL15534 / VA0x140213340 compares each coordinate against plus/minus stored values; OBB creation AL15535 / VA0x1402133C0 copies them unchanged. Thus raw XPRM bounds are half-extents on this load path, not full widths. The alternate factory AL15520 / VA0x140212BD0 halves input but is not used by this loader. Inset40 puts thin-axis center offset45.051 near half-extent44, preserving actual-contact validation. This resolves Reviewer A's full-width caveat; no live probe added.

- Native AL75619 / VA140FC2390 setter readback now verifies BOTH save and character-data paths; AL109356 / VA14159CA60 and AL109357 / VA14159CAC0 consume them. Failure prevents farm connection. Ignore the hotkey only in farm mode, reject missing registry paths, and centralize no-modal Die behavior in Utils/Error.cpp because ExeLoader also calls it directly. Preserve error text under the private farm root.
- PresentMon source documentation (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/GameTechDev/PresentMon/main/README-ConsoleApplication.md) defines --qpc_time_ms. CPython 3.12 source (https://raw.githubusercontent.com/python/cpython/v3.12.0/Python/pytime.c, py_get_win_perf_counter) converts the raw QPC counter without subtracting a process-local origin. Adopt perf_counter milliseconds for cross-process ETW coverage and calibrate GetTickCount64 for native frame records; persist clock-calibration.json. Endpoint-only coverage was rejected by fault injection: clustered endpoints can conceal a missing middle. Require a present within one second of every native frame; missing native frames during a real stall do not falsely imply lost ETW.
- ProcDump cancellation is a request, not completion (https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/sysinternals/downloads/procdump). Wait for the actual helper exit before inspecting final logs/dumps; retain job-assigned suspended targets on partial diagnostic startup failure. Full launch-path and delayed-evidence tests cover these failure paths.
- Independent reviews: A review initially REFUTED concrete launch/teardown/evidence defects; resolutions are in C:/Tools/skyrim_re/farm/REVIEW_RESOLUTION.md.
