## Ragdollfix round 1: refutation disposition and root-frame conclusion (2026-09-27)

The complete `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/conductor/ragdollfix-verify0.log` was read. **The acceptance refutation stands:** no paired evidence establishes the dynamic patch's moving/settled targets or runtime non-regression. The original task prohibits this worker from running the games. No xmake build, deployment, game/process control or commit occurred. Full point-by-point disposition and per-file changes: [RAGDOLL_STEERING.md](RAGDOLL_STEERING.md).

Re-ran historical measurement over 534 authority captures with 32 processes: six comparable dead-actor samples. The latest Lokir sample (`authority-20260926-140905.json`, actor654FB, 11ms sample skew) has 18 dynamic host bodies (types2/3) and 18 keyframed follower bodies (type4). Its 0.0007u body error is a forbidden-keyframe artifact, not dynamic-steering validation. The revised JSON explicitly labels this. Moving sample maximum remains475.31438u/158.94260deg, rendered352.62179u/153.53455deg; it does not identify an initial fall trajectory.

**New conclusion, HIGH confidence within the sampled nodes:** the settled rendered mismatch is a rigid root-frame discrepancy. Its99 evaluated local transforms agree to0.0000251u/0.0000377deg; captured root displacement is47.22780u and root rotation error44.79557deg. Transforming each sampled world node back into its own captured root frame, independently of every articulated node, reduces maximum residual to0.0135583u/0.0000253deg. All12 samples close; only three are articulated and nine are duplicate root placeholders. This numerically explains the78.36549u/44.79557deg world-pose gap at that instant. It does not identify a writer, prove named-bone correspondence, or validate the current build. `Measure-RagdollBaseline.py::root_closure` records both articulated-only and all-sample results. Tests reject independent articulated errors and a non-root anchor.

Public primary-source search repeated for [PLANCK](https://github.com/adamhynek/activeragdoll/blob/master/src/main.cpp) and [HIGGS](https://github.com/adamhynek/higgs/blob/master/src/physics.cpp). Inspected local PLANCK `PostPostPhysicsHook` (both pose and world-from-model tracks), `src/main.cpp:6147` (inverse wrapper-T), `include/RE/havok_behavior.h:831` (track bounds); HIGGS `src/physics.cpp:865` (COM-aware velocity and locked fallback). Adopt native mapping plus paired root/pose retention. Reject blindly importing wrapper-T compensation, mapper constraints or a root teleport: the supplied sample closes as a rigid frame discrepancy, and these sources do not establish its causal writer. No new pose intervention was justified solely by the old capture.

Native source/assembly reinspection (all VAs preferred, same executable):

| VA / Address Library ID | Evidence and disposition |
|---|---|
| `0x140AC8B80 / 58293`, `0x140ACA000 / 58308`, `0x140ACA440 / 58311` | Readback gates, current-vs-swept body sampling, skeleton mapping and world-from-model pairing. Preserve these native transformations; body index is not rendered-bone index. |
| `0x140AC6CC0 / 58287` | Writes driver+CA=1 and returns driver+90. Typed `skx.py --typed offin 0xca id:58293 w` identified this callee, disproving a provisional concern that the existing post-readback CA gate could never pass. No flag change made. |
| `0x140BCAB40 / 63563`, `0x140BCB640 / 63569` | Graph postPhysics reads back locals, sets world-from-model through native delta notification, then copies bones. This is why paired root/bone state is retained. It does not prove every later node writer honors that state. |
| `0x140B9A9C0 / 62478` | Assembly confirms RCX position, RDX aligned quaternion, XMM2 inverse step, R9 body. Reads local COM+1F0/currentCOM+1C0/orientation+1E0, activates and invokes velocity slots. Existing QsTransform is alignas(16); the function itself does not keyframe motion. |
| `0x140B5B0B0 / 61410`, `0x140B5B250 / 61417`, `0x14104AC30 / 77851` | Both step branches are distinct. Multithreaded step waits on the job pool and calls its end phase before return. Current corpse hooks steer pre-call, observe/correct post-return. No double-steering claim. |
| `0x14104FCE0 / 77928`, `0x14104FD00 / 77929` | Conditional wrapper lock/unlock+C598, not Havok critical-operation counters. Reflection via hkq confirms world F0/F4/F8/100 and their separate meanings. Live same-world serialization remains an assumption requiring confirmation. |
| `0x140B4CEF0 / 60898`, `0x140B4DCD0 / 60918` | Pose changes before collision refresh; refresh can run callbacks and drain pending mutations before return. Added authority/world/UID/idle rechecks between members and after the transaction; interrupted placement never reports exact success. There is no claim of an atomic full-set broadphase update. |
| `0x140B4B0C0 / 60850` | Whole-island deactivation; native reads body+130, island members+60/count+68. Added membership exclusion for any unrelated island member, not merely a loose speed check. Shared Resting now requires <0.03 Havok units/s and <0.05rad/s on both PCs, rejecting NaNs. Existing constraints and door audit observers/pass-through remain. |

`StepBinding` shared_ptr retains allocation, not logical membership or thread safety. Mutable step fields are protected by the native same-world step phase and wrapper lock; indexed invalidation and UID checks remain necessary. The tighter rest threshold may prevent exact fallback under ongoing contacts. Setters remain callback-capable, and source checks cannot rule out every producer of stale world membership. These limitations are explicit, not resolved by matching reviewer opinions.

Scale correction: with A_total distinct corpses, capture O(A_total B), relay O((P-1)A_total B); with A corpses per each of P owners, relay becomes O(P squared A B). Capture revisits roughly ceil(A_owner/32)*33ms; publication ceil(streams/64)*33ms; solver visits at most64 streams x64 bodies, so large cohorts receive fewer steps. Sleep ownership exclusion costs at most I*128*B comparisons on a settle attempt, I<=B<=64. There is no five/ten-player CPU/memory/transport measurement and no blanket zero-allocation claim for existing deque scheduling.

Fresh isolated checks: MSVC /Zs passed seven task units; protocol3445 assertions/four cases; capture20 tests. A final corpse /Zs recheck follows the callback recheck edits. Compiler setup printed a vswhere PATH warning but resolved the existing MSVC toolchain and completed. Searching available JSONL captures found no ragdoll_error/ragdoll_render records; searching captured logs found no dynamic residual lines. Earlier readback-publication lines (for example `harness-20260927-123343/Follower/tp_client.1.log:320,25172`) do not establish changed dynamic behavior. Historical log scan now reads173 files,16643 unique old post-placement residuals and326 exact-settle lines; counts can grow as the coordinator adds captures. No continuous fall, changed-build target, persistence/impact/recovery/execution/cell/door/reload/handoff, or performance pass is claimed.

## Meteor debris: impact hazards (2026-09-28)

Owner report: Alduin's Helgen meteors line up on both PCs, the debris after each impact does not.

Records (Skyrim.esm, read from the plugin with `C:\Tools\skyrim_re\agent\recdump.py`): PROJ D4888 FireboltStormProjectileSlow01 explodes with EXPL EA517 (no placed object, no hazard). The spell effect MGEF E9AC7 names impact data set IPDS D07C2 MAGFirebolt01WithRocksImpactSet; on stone, dirt, snow, gravel and similar materials it plays IPCT D07C1 MAGFireSpellmpactROCKS01, whose NAM2 places HAZD D07BC FXHavokRockHazard (5 s lifetime, align-to-normal). Other materials place HAZD 3E075/3E073. `Effects\FXHavokRockHazard.nif` (extracted with `agent\bsa_extract.py`, listed with `agent\nif_blocks.py`): BSFadeNode root without collision, ten DirtClod NiNodes each with bhkRigidBodyT + bhkCollisionObject.

| VA / Address Library ID | Evidence |
|---|---|
| `0x1407EDAB0 / 43954` | Hazard::Create. Input: base +0x00, cell +0x08, position +0x10, normal +0x1C, actor cause +0x38 (first dword = owner handle), lifetime +0x40, radius +0x44, ignore-interval +0x4C, permanent +0x4D. Refuses on fHazardMinimumSpawnInterval unless +0x4C. Allocates 0xE0 Hazard (vtable 0x1419405D8). |
| `0x140801A50` | Projectile impact effect: BSTempEffectParticle spawn, BGSImpactManager::PlayImpactDataSounds, then Hazard::Create from the impact data's hazard (+0x80); owner from the projectile's GetActorCause (vtable slot 0x51). Return address 0x140802205 in logs. |
| `0x1401BC1C0 / 13723` | CreateReferenceAtLocation also reaches Hazard::Create (a PlaceAtMe of a HAZD makes a hazard). |

Measurement (probe on Hazard::Create, both PCs, helgen_postcreator run): 184 of 184 host hazards had a follower hazard of the same base within 0-5 u, identical normal, same frame (the relayed world-caster projectile); every one had force=1 so the interval never refused. Spawning is therefore already in sync; the clods are free Havok bodies that part ways.

Change: the leader streams each live hazard's rigid bodies (tree order, root body + relative children) on the leader physics lane under GameId mod UINT32_MAX-2; Rotation carries the base's server GameId and the leader-side age. The follower binds to its own hazard of the same base within 20 u and 1000 ms, and publishes one dynamic StepTarget per clod (exact drive E62478 while the host body moves). Switch `hazard_sync` (off = bind and measure only).

Paired A/B (follower toggled every 15 s during the attack, `agent\ab_hazard.ps1` + `agent\hazard_ab.py`): per-hazard mean clod gap to the host sample, median 170.3 u unsteered (n=137) vs 3.2 u steered (n=173); steered p90 5.9 u. A first build bound by position only and cross-bound 58 of 229 streams to body-less hazards of another base; matching base and age removed them (0 of 199). Reviewer B refutation: SHIP WITH FIXES; applied base/age matching, 20 u radius, quaternion-norm checks and clearing on an inactive session. Not done: send-rate reduction (bursts of about 15 live hazards x 9 bodies at 20 Hz until rest, roughly 110 KB/s leader to server during a shower), follower-owned hazards (the leader lane accepts leader updates only).

## Animation-graph props and settled corpse authority (2026-09-28)

Owner reports: the Helgen headsman has no axe on the follower; Lokir's corpse reappeared on the follower and was thrown around by the meteors while the host had none.

Props (read from the exe; Reviewer B's diag-animobj agrees): behavior events `AnimationObjectLoad.<EDID>` / `AnimationObjectDraw.<EDID>` reach AnimationObjectLoadHandler / AnimationObjectDrawHandler slot 1 (`0x1407CF070 / 42888`, `0x1407CF0F0 / 42889`), which resolve the payload with LookupByEditorID (`0x1401E5910 / 14618`, form type 0x53 ANIO) and call the AnimationObjects manager (singleton `400328`, RVA 0x219E5C8, read from the store in its constructor `0x1407D7CE0 / 43569`): Load `0x1407D7DC0 / 43571` (0x28-byte entry: handle, ANIO, node, load task, draw-pending byte; spinlock +0x20), Draw `0x1407D8080 / 43572` (clears node +0xF4 bit 0), Detach `0x1407D88F0 / 43577` (matches the ANIO's unload event name at +0x60), teardown on 3D release `0x1407D82B0 / 43573`, save restore `0x1407D8DA0`. A remote copy follows the owner's pose and never plays the clip, and a checkpoint load restores entries without events, so hooking the handlers alone logged nothing in the post-creator run. Change: owners send live handler events plus a one-second manager snapshot of their owned actors (new or changed props, detaches for vanished ones, everything every 10 s); copies apply Load/Draw/Detach on the main thread. Two-PC run: AnimObjectExecutionerAxe (2E8E5) load/draw and later detach applied on the follower's headsman; cuffs F1BFF/F1C00 on prisoners and players; Hadvar's EA339/EB058. Reviewer B refutation: SHIP WITH FIXES; applied detach, main-thread apply, ownership tokens, ANIO type check on receive, coalescing, factory test.

Corpse authority: `RagdollRelayState` pinned authority while any limb stream was active, including settled ones. At a checkpoint load the follower registered the long-dead Lokir first and started streaming him (16:06:17), so the leader's claim (16:06:18, `claim=true`) was refused in `TransferOwnership` for good; the host never bound its Lokir (`Pending leader assignment`, `waiting (no actor for server id)`) and the follower simulated him locally. Change: only a limb still in motion pins; a transfer of a settled corpse ends the old owner's streams first (`EndRagdollStreams`). Next run: `Ragdoll server 13: settled streams ended for an ownership transfer to player 1`, host streaming Lokir.

## Revive/wipe review fixes and creator slider audit (2026-09-29)

Adversarial review of acc758a1..e74a8715 (REFUTED); each point was read against the exe before changing code.

- Face tint builder `FaceGenSystem::CreateTints 0x14043C530 / 27040`: writes every layer with alpha > 0 into a pass of 0x20-byte slots at the job base, pads the unused ones up to 0x10, and has no upper bound; a 17th visible layer lands on the field at +0x200. The player's own creator uses the same builder (`52396 / 0x14096BE70`, "Player face tint", render target 0xF), so a vanilla character never has more than 16 visible layers and the receiver's 16-layer cap drops nothing vanilla can show.
- RaceSex menu slider callbacks (registered in `0x140968C50`): ChangeRace 52351, ChangeSex 52352, ChangeWeight 52353, ChangeFaceDetails 52354, ChangeMorph 52355, ChangeDoubleMorph 52356, ChangeHeadPart 52357, ChangePreset 52358, ChangeHeadPreset 52359, ChangeHairColorPreset 52360, ChangeTintingMask 52361 (skin tone: `TESNPC::SetSkinFromTint` then `27059 / 0x14043D430`, which writes the skin color into the player's feature-5 materials in place), ChangeMask 52362, ChangeMaskColor 52363. Args: FxDelegateArgs handler +0x18, GFxValue args +0x28 (0x18 each, number at +0x10), count +0x30. Slider array: menu+0x140[menu+0x198], entry (0x28) at menu+0x188, data +8, size +0x18; slider 0x138 bytes (min +0, max +4, callback name +0x20, tint type +0x124, value +0x130). creator_slide_test drives all of them: no slider, race or sex change touches the other player's character on either PC.
- `ShoutHandler` button slot (`0x1407B3960 / 42430`, vtable ShoutHandler slot 6): ButtonEvent value +0x28, held seconds +0x2C, confirmed in the decompile. The ritual's capture now hands the key back (press, then live events) when the ritual disarms mid-hold.
- `Game.SetCameraTarget 0x1408FB250 / 50838` writes PlayerCamera+0x3C (handle or null handle); `0x1408F9EB0 / 50824` swaps the camera from the state at +0x110 back to third person (+0xE8).
- Papyrus `Actor.IsInCombat 0x140A0D6D0` is only `Actor vtable +0x718`; `Actor::IsInCombat` calls that directly.
- Regression found and fixed: native registrations went through `RunnerService::Trigger`, whose load generation (136344f8) drops events queued before a game load, so PapyrusService's table stayed empty and every name-bound Papyrus call returned a default (IsInCombat, GetCombatTarget, PlaceAtMe, RemoveAllItems...). Registrations now write the table directly under a lock. Gate: agent\papyrus_check.ps1 (672 natives registered on 1.7.104; every PAPYRUS_FUNCTION name in the source must resolve). Game.SetInChargen never appears in that hook; call `55576` directly.
- Remote stand-up check: a stop that plays but leaves a copy's graph in its down states is caught by comparing the active state machines' (node, state) digest sampled while down with the digest 1.5 s after standing (then IdleForceDefaultState). IsBleedingOut / iState are replicated from the owner, so they cannot show the copy's own graph state.
- Head-tracking variables owned by the receiver are resolved by name from the graph's variable table (`BShkbHkxDB` animation variable map) once per graph type: 12 of 12 on Master_Behavior, and the same names on beast and modded graphs.

## Face tint render target, grab, corpse layer, off-thread moves (2026-09-29, afternoon)

- Tint job runner `0x14043C9D0`: walks the queued job list in order; for each job draws image space effect 99 into render target 0xF (`0x1414EE1C0(ImageSpaceManager, 99, 0xF)`), then, only if the job has a target texture (+0x280), gives it a copy of 0xF (`0x14100EF50`: new texture + context CopyResource). A job with a null target therefore only redraws 0xF. The creator's tint path (`52396 / 0x14096BE70`) points the player's face texture buffer at `0x14100DD60(BSGraphics, 0xF)`: a shader resource view on 0xF itself (device vfunc +0x38), i.e. live. Every copy's tint job therefore painted the other player onto the local face; the fix queues one local `CreateTints(visible local tints <= 16, nullptr)` after the frame's copy jobs. GPU readback of the local face (creator_slide_test -Control): 19 host changes altered it without the redraw, 0 with it.
- Grab: `PlayerCharacter::StartGrabObject 0x140751870 / 40552` reads CrosshairPickData (`401585`, target handle +4), checks mass, then calls `0x140752630 / 40555 (player, ref, 1, 1000.0, 0)`; `PlayerCharacter::DestroyMouseSprings 0x140754110 / 40557`. The grab update `0x140753580` releases at `0x140753A91` when grab type != 2 and |object - grab point| (Havok) x 69.99 (`0x1417F423C`) > 96.0 (`0x14181AB88`).
- Dead body collision layer: the death path `0x1406A9E50 / 37896` calls `NiAVObject::SetCollisionLayer 0x141053280 / 77998 (node, 0x20)` only when `flags1 & 0xE000000` is 0 or 0xC000000 (knock state 0 or 6) and the ragdoll flag check passes; `AIProcess::KnockExplosion 39895` also sets 0x20; `Actor::ClearDeathState` sets 8. A copy killed by sync mid-ragdoll (knock 1) stayed on layer 8. The call recurses: ref_bodies read every ragdoll body at layer 32 after one call on the root (corpse_test).
- Off-thread moves: network messages are handled in the client update, run from the Papyrus VM update hook (off the main thread). A teleport notice made `MoveActor` call `TESWorldSpace::LoadCell 20460` there and the host aborted in the CRT (dump SkyrimTogether.exe.67508, 2026-09-29 15:30). Moves requested off the main thread are queued and applied from `HookMainLoop`.
# Starter, release archive, and installer research (2026-09-30)

- The upstream [TiltedEvolution source tree](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedEvolution) includes an immersive launcher. This fork's `Code/immersive_launcher/Launcher.cpp` and `oobe/PathSelection.cpp` were inspected: the launcher loads Skyrim into its own process and expects the mod folder layout. A separate tiny starter was adopted so update files can be replaced before those imports are loaded. Reusing the immersive launcher as the updater was rejected because it cannot reliably replace its own loaded binaries.
- The [Tilted Phoques launcher guide](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/gitbook-wiki/blob/main/guides/client-setup/using-vortex-mod-manager-vmm/skyrim-together-reborn/launching-skyrimtogether-through-vmm.md) gives the installed `Data/SkyrimTogetherReborn/SkyrimTogether.exe` location and `-r` argument. The starter stays beside that executable and forwards the original command-line tail.
- The existing `Code/client/Services/Generic/UpdateService.cpp` was inspected for the exact `pending.json` and `ready` contract. The starter validates the same Data roots, staged hashes, and version before writing. The existing service was left untouched as requested.
- [Microsoft MoveFileExW documentation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winbase/nf-winbase-movefileexw) documents replacement and write-through flags. These were adopted for each staged sibling and journal replacement. A multi-file update is not atomic, so the starter journals and backs up each file.
- [GitHub's generate release notes endpoint](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/releases/releases#generate-release-notes-content-for-a-release) accepts a tag before a release exists. The release workflow generates notes once and passes the same file to both the manifest and release creation.
- [Inno Setup's official Pascal scripting reference](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_scriptfunctions.htm) provides the registry, file version, and file-list operations used for game detection and plugins.txt. A broad delete-on-uninstall list was rejected: the install log owns only the files in the staged payload.

Reviewer A (read-only) found that the old workflows still targeted `master` although this fork has `origin/main`, and that crash rollback could try to replace the running starter. The workflows now target `main`; rollback renames the running image before restoring its backup. Reviewer A also identified cleanup exceptions, an abandoned mutex result, flat PDB copy collisions, and elevated Inno access to the wrong user profile. Cleanup uses nonthrowing operations after commit, abandoned mutex ownership is accepted, PDB paths are preserved, and the installer runs in the invoking user's context and checks Data write access. Its report also flagged that `UpdateService.cpp` serializes a non-ASCII payload path through the ANSI code page. That in-game file is outside this task's permitted edits, so the non-ASCII profile case remains unresolved.

The [Inno Setup administrative install mode documentation](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_admininstallmode.htm) says per-user settings must not be edited from an elevated installer. A per-user install was adopted to ensure Steam detection and `plugins.txt` use the actual player's profile. This means a protected Skyrim Data folder cannot be installed without changing its permissions or moving the Steam library; the installer stops with a clear message. The [Microsoft redistributable download](https://aka.ms/vs/17/release/vc_redist.x64.exe) is Authenticode-checked for a valid Microsoft signer before bundling.

This is source and documentation review only. The starter, release workflow, and installer have not been built, installed, or exercised in-game, so no runtime issue is claimed fixed. Reviewer B was unavailable because its auth settings file could not be read. No engine function VA or Address Library ID is applicable: no Skyrim engine offset, diagnostic counter, or live probe was added.

## b1 crash and thread safety batch (2026-10-02)

Historical round-0 notes below are superseded by **ov-b1 round 1: independent refutation resolved** later in this file. In particular, duplicate-form events are no longer dropped, the new pending-replay timeout was withdrawn, and cost/runtime claims were narrowed.

Primary source inspected: [SKSE64 Hooks_Threads.cpp](https://github.com/ianpatt/skse64/blob/master/skse64/Hooks_Threads.cpp) locks its task queue for insertion, then drains tasks on an engine phase. This supports queueing from native hooks, but its drain phase is later than this mod's X.00 mailbox, so the existing HookMainLoop was used for menu, weather and classification work. [PrismaUI MainThreadQueue.cpp](https://github.com/PrismaUI-SKSE/framework/blob/main/src/Utils/MainThreadQueue.cpp) likewise swaps a protected queue before running UI work; its input event pump was rejected because it does not cover the title and load cases here. [TiltedCore TaskQueue.hpp](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedCore/blob/master/Code/core/include/TaskQueue.hpp) and [TaskQueue.cpp](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedCore/blob/master/Code/core/src/TaskQueue.cpp) confirm this mod's RunnerService::Trigger reaches a mutex protected Add. Drain holds that mutex while executing callbacks, so the queue is thread safe for producer insertion but recursive enqueue from a drained callback would deadlock; no such enqueue was added by this batch. [CommonLibSSE-NG UI.h](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/include/RE/U/UI.h) identifies numItemMenus at UI+0x164. [UIMessageQueue.h](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/include/RE/U/UIMessageQueue.h) shows the finite message pool. The native functions below were checked with `sk.py id` followed by `sk.py fn <VA>`; source descriptions alone were not used to choose offsets.

| Item | Native evidence checked | Change and remaining limit |
|---|---|
| FRAME-28 | VM job `0x1406E57D0 / 39074` calls VM update `0x1409D7450 / 53926`; main loop `0x140658870 / 36564` is the existing X.00 hook. | Busy replay, trigger classification and weather mutation now have X.00 paths. The rest of World::Update still executes on the VM job, so this item remains partial; no blanket thread safety claim. |
| QUEST-26 | Physics trigger update `0x1404066C0 / 26036`; Papyrus ForEachBoundObject `0x1414B2600 / 104791` takes the attached script lock, and alias visitor `0x1402E6A70 / 19656` reaches alias locking. | Hold now uses cached classification only. A cache miss waits conservatively; at most four pending handles are classified per main frame, without the gate mutex held. Rejected classifying inside the physics callback. |
| ITEMS-05 | Equip queue consumer `0x140720D90 / 39814`, job dispatcher `0x1406E5DB0 / 39089`. | Component signals maintain a mutex protected form ID to ownership token index. Job hooks read that map in O(1), not an entt view. Ambiguous duplicate form IDs drop rather than choose a possibly wrong owner. The existing runner handler still checks the captured epoch before sending, so a later ownership change drops the event. TiltedCore's Add uses its own mutex; its Drain locking scope remains a separate reentrancy risk. |
| FRAME-15 | ContainerMenu::OpenMenu `0x140911FB0 / 51140` tests UI state, checks pending messages `0x14116E0D0 / 82168`, then posts through AddMessage `0x1401B47F0 / 13631`. | All three Replay callers now queue a request ID for X.00. Container/barter replays wait while UI+0x164 is nonzero; container also waits for a queued show. A pending lease keeps heartbeats and cancels after 15 s if the menu stays blocked. Rejected replay from the VM worker. |
| FRAME-14 | Load function `0x1401A6540 / 13363` can enter the loading wait and UI work; AddMessage `0x1401B47F0 / 13631` manages pool slots without an outer producer lock. | The load hook sets one atomic splash restoration flag. OnMainFrame posts the two menus on the next frame. |
| FRAME-27 | Message pump `0x14065C3E0 / 36607` is reached from loading wait `0x14060E9C0 / 35407`; load byte at `0x14219E5F6 / 400336`. | WndProc retains wake requests during a load and drains them in X.00 after the load byte clears. This avoids renderer resize in the loading message pump. |
| FRAME-01 | ForceWeather `0x140415540 / 26243` first calls ResetWeather `0x1404154C0 / 26242`, which releases cloud and precipitation resources via `0x14040CCE0 / 26151` and `0x14040F3C0 / 26180`. | Sky updates run from X.00. A 2 Hz host blend refresh does not reforce the same current weather; a new ID or actual current weather mismatch can. |
| FRAME-24 | Renderer::End `0x141009F70 / 77246` can be reached from Bink `0x1411B2050 / 90259`; Renderer::Lock `0x141009CF0 / 77243` enters the critical section at renderer+0x27F0. | The hook reads End's caller return slot at +0x50, identifies the Bink return at base+0x23C, try-enters the renderer lock and skips a busy frame. Worker callers draw the last ImGui frame and CEF without running UI callbacks; main render callers retain the existing UI and GFx logic. Moving all normal render reads to X.00 snapshots remains open. |

New recurring cost: O(1) per main frame for busy, lobby, weather and deferred window flags; O(1) for each native ownership lookup and each component signal update, with O(A) token storage; at most four uncached held triggers classified per main frame, each with the existing 64 step activation traversal cap. Trigger classification work is event queued rather than a per frame pending scan. No new per frame actor or body scan and no P squared loop was added. Existing TriggerGate::OnUpdate scans its party and held trigger state; that work was not expanded. No changed build or paired two PC run was performed in this batch. Reviewer A and Reviewer B results are recorded separately when available; agreement cannot substitute for a paired observation.

Independent review: Reviewer A found that clearing `m_waiting` when queuing BusyLockService::Replay lets the same World update cancel it before X.00 because `m_openDeadline` is still zero. OnUpdate now returns while a valid replay is pending, after the existing load/party invalidation checks. Reviewer A also found that a leader's cached sky state could suppress the initial party weather send; party join now invalidates `m_sentSky` before the X.00 send. Its duplicate form ID concern was resolved by making the ownership index retain entity keyed entries and drop ambiguous lookups. It noted that an uncached quest trigger is delayed by at least one frame; this is the chosen conservative miss policy and remains a test risk. It suggested removing the Renderer+0x27F0 offset because it could not verify it; `Renderer::Lock 0x141009CF0 / 77243` was read and directly enters that offset, so the offset was retained. Other lobby observations were outside the requested eight items. Reviewer B was asked twice, but its first attempt stopped at the step limit and the second could not read the workspace because its Windows sandbox ACL setup failed (`SetNamedSecurityInfoW 1340`); it did not review the code.

Reviewer A's follow-up found the BusyLock replay survives its next OnUpdate and preserves heartbeats, and checked the 82168 calling convention. It also found the initial leader weather send is no longer suppressed. The follow-up incorrectly stated that OnNotify and OnUpdate are main-thread calls: the `0x1406E57D0 / 39074` to `0x1409D7450 / 53926` job path above shows running-frame World updates on a worker. The plain mailbox fields rely on X.00 running before the VM job starts and on the existing serialized World dispatch, not on a same-thread assumption. Paired testing and build validation remain necessary.


## ov-b1 round 1: independent refutation resolved (2026-10-02)

Historical round-1 conclusions below are superseded by **ov-b1 round 2: verify1 resolution**. In particular, ITEMS-05 is only partly fixed; drain-side worker registry access and O(E*N) cost remain. The obsolete scale-test equality assertion is now reconciled and the integrated suite passes.

Read all of `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/conductor/ov-b1-verify0.log`. The earlier ITEMS-05 claim omitted a real race in ActorExtension flags; broad regression and scale claims were not justified. The revised full report, per-item evidence, test results, next-run observations and risks are in `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/overnight/report-b1.md`.

**Conclusion:** the round-0 refutation was justified. Its concrete ownership, replay-cancellation and backlog defects are corrected in source, but the aggregate crash/thread-safety goal remains open. FRAME-28 still has worker engine mutation outside the three moved paths, and FRAME-24 still reads UI/GFx/player state in normal main rendering. This is a high-confidence source conclusion, not an unanswered instrumentation question. No changed plugin build or two-PC run exists; no observed crash is claimed fixed. COMMON.md prohibits xmake builds and waives the two local reviewers for this task. No new independent assistant review is claimed.

Primary sources searched and inspected:

- [SKSE64 Hooks_Threads.cpp](https://github.com/ianpatt/skse64/blob/master/skse64/Hooks_Threads.cpp): producer insertion is protected and the consumer releases its queue lock before calling a task. Adopted the general producer/consumer separation; retained this mod's X.00 phase instead of moving work to SKSE's later engine hook. No blanket main-thread claim is made about this mod's RunnerService.
- [TiltedCore v0.2.9 TaskQueue.cpp](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedCore/blob/v0.2.9/Code/core/src/TaskQueue.cpp), retrieved directly through raw.githubusercontent.com when the browsing fetch failed, and the installed `TiltedCore/TaskQueue.hpp`: `Add` locks its mutex, confirming producer safety for the exact dependency version selected by xmake.lua. `Drain` holds that mutex across callbacks. Adopted the existing producer queue; rejected asserting that this also makes registry access safe, makes callbacks run on main, or permits recursive enqueue. Recursive `Add` during `Drain` remains a separate reentrancy risk.
- [CommonLibSSE-NG UI.h](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/include/RE/U/UI.h): `numItemMenus` at +0x164 and `numDisablePauseMenu` at +0x168. Adopted these typed fields with native validation below; rejected using +0x164 alone as proof that ContainerMenu::OpenMenu will accept a request.
- Installed [EnTT v3.10.0 view.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/view.hpp), [sigh_storage_mixin.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/sigh_storage_mixin.hpp) and [storage.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/storage.hpp): inspected pool choice, reverse iteration, destruction signal timing, and swap-and-pop. Adopted copied pool sizes/ranks maintained on component signals, so a duplicate native form ID selects exactly the old first match without worker EnTT access. Rejected the round-0 drop-all-duplicates policy because it changed baseline event selection. Zero-epoch entries remain candidates and reject only when selected, matching baseline. Explicit pool sorting would require a new publication path; no client sort calls were found.

### Exact evidence and revisions

`Actor::GetExtension`/`AsExActor`/`AsExPlayerCharacter` at Actor.cpp:292-320 only check form type/player identity and compute the embedded extension pointer (References.h ExActor/ExPlayerCharacter inheritance). Thus the pointer accessor itself is not a registry lookup. The unsynchronized field was `ActorExtension::onlineFlags`. It is now `std::atomic<uint32_t>`; its methods use relaxed loads and bitwise atomic RMWs, and combined remote/player predicates use one load. This preserves the six equip hooks' synchronous authority/override decisions, including the inventory-override unequip escape. It does not synchronize the extension's other fields, publish registry objects, or protect dead actor pointers. Native worker evidence remains equip consumer **0x140720D90 / 39814**, called at **0x1406E5F5E** in **0x1406E5DB0 / 39089**.

`OwnershipTokenIndex` now publishes first-match ranks rather than dropping duplicates. Only signal callbacks access EnTT; native getters lock the copied map. `InventoryService::OnInventoryChangeEvent` at :76-89 and `OnEquipmentChangeEvent` at :146-151 already validate captured ServerId/OwnershipEpoch. This disproves the review's failure to find drain checks. `World.cpp:261` and `RunnerService::OnUpdate` show that drain belongs to World dispatch, which may run on the worker: **0x1406E57D0 / 39074** calls **0x1409D7450 / 53926**. The map approach is a deliberate variation on the published-map recipe, avoiding a per-frame full registry copy. It does not serialize all registry writers.

The new BusyLock 15-second pending cancellation was removed, including its timestamp. Valid requests keep heartbeats while another menu blocks them; lifecycle/load/death/party checks still invalidate them. OnMainFrame now rechecks CanReplay before waiting. **ContainerMenu::OpenMenu 0x140911FB0 / 51140** calls **0x1411696E0 / 82076**, which reads `UI+0x168`, then **0x14116E0D0 / 82168**, whose operands are queue, pointer to BSFixedString, and integer message type. The pending-message routine locks queue+0x10 and compares the message type and interned name. The new precondition includes `numDisablePauseMenu` for containers; native refusal must not consume the pending replay. Existing post-open deadlines and lockpicking behavior were retained. **AddMessage 0x1401B47F0 / 13631** still owns its unprotected pool allocation counter; X.00 phase remains necessary.

Trigger classification still uses the four-request X.00 budget, avoiding **0x1414B2600 / 104791** attached-script lock +0x93A0 and **0x1402E6A70 / 19656** -> **0x140187920 / 12720** alias locking within **0x1404066C0 / 26036** physics-trigger delivery. Queue entries now have list iterators owned by held contacts: release/cancel removes their work in O(1). Results require matching generation, form ID and hold-order number, preventing reuse of an old classification result for a replacement contact. The deferred quest/scene barrier guard's eight seconds now begin at release time, so time spent queued does not exhaust that window before delivery. Cache-miss latency/global event reordering remains a real risk; no fixed live-backlog cap is claimed.

Renderer offsets were independently verified with Capstone on `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/SkyrimSE-1.7.104.exe`, not inferred from a CommonLib layout:

| Native | Exact static evidence |
|---|---|
| **End 0x141009F70 / 77246** | Starts `48 83 EC 48` (sub rsp, 0x48). The swapped call is at 0x141009F79; its next instruction is 0x141009F7E. The hook return slot is 8+0x48 below End's own caller slot, proving +0x50. |
| **Bink frame 0x1411B2050 / 90259** | Call at 0x1411B2287 has bytes `E8 E4 7C E5 FF` and targets End; return is 0x1411B228C, exactly function+0x23C. |
| **Renderer::Lock 0x141009CF0 / 77243** | `48 81 C1 F0 27 00 00` adds +0x27F0 before tail-jumping to EnterCriticalSection. |

These offsets are statically proven for this executable. They have not been captured from the changed live hook. Worker draws skip this mod's overlay under renderer/mod-lock contention but always continue to StopTimer/native End. That can omit overlay frames and is not rendering equivalence. Worker loading calls intentionally use the same reduced draw path. Normal main rendering still performs engine/GFx work; the downstream CEF renderer still has blocking texture locks and resource/paint maintenance. Consequently FRAME-24 remains partial, and 'draw-only' does not mean wholly wait-free downstream code.

The remaining batch evidence was re-read with `sk.py fn id:...`: main loop **0x140658870 / 36564**; load **0x1401A6540 / 13363** and UI pool cleanup **0x14116E030 / 82167**; message pump **0x14065C3E0 / 36607** in loading wait **0x14060E9C0 / 35407**; load byte **0x14219E5F6 / 400336** independently mapped with `id`. Weather ForceWeather **0x140415540 / 26243** calls ResetWeather **0x1404154C0 / 26242**, releasing cloud/precipitation resources through **0x14040CCE0 / 26151**, **0x14040F3C0 / 26180**. Weather's unchanged-current-ID refresh avoids reforce, but a real local mismatch can reforce the same host ID; 'at most once per host change' was too absolute.

### Regression and validation limits

Compared affected paths with commit `25fa030d` and the pre-revision working diff. Preserved native equip suppression, epoch validation, trigger authority/contact/barrier rules, Busy fallback/heartbeats/post-open deadlines, and the existing ragdoll/execution/cart/door-vote paths. Only this batch's duplicate-drop policy and pending-cancel behavior were withdrawn. Kept unrelated live lobby, party, character-screen, input and diagnostic changes. These comparisons do not establish in-game timing equivalence.

MSVC `/Zs` passed for 14 relevant production translation units, including ActorExtension, Utils, Busy, Trigger, renderer, Weather, Lobby, ImGui, Overlay, RenderSystem, SkyrimVM64, Actor, EquipManager and TESObjectREFR. Source hashes were unchanged across each syntax check. Actual ActorExtension methods passed all flag combinations and 20,000 synchronized concurrent bit updates. Current extracted OwnershipTokenIndex with actual EnTT passed 20,000 randomized mutation steps, comparing 17 keys for both roles after every step against baseline view iteration (680,000 comparisons, plus bootstrap/clear checks); duplicate/zero-epoch/swap/driver-change cases were included. Five and ten reader threads also passed with 10,000 writer mutations each; these are concurrency tests, not multiplayer benchmarks. Existing trigger tests passed 52 assertions in seven cases. An extracted production OnMainFrame test passed its four-item budget, cancellation ticket removal, replaced-contact and generation checks with native stubs. `git -c core.safecrlf=false diff --check` passed. No schema or packet code changed, no plugin was linked, and no paired game observation was made. Scratch test artifacts are in `C:/Users/mwalt/AppData/Local/Temp/ov-b1-round1-n2dn_2qs/`.

The separate scale batch's `Code/tests/run_scale_checks.py` currently asserts that generic ownership helper text equals `25fa030d`; that literal assertion conflicts with this authorized b1 change. It was neither edited nor used as passing evidence. The coordinator must reconcile it with the baseline-equivalence tests instead of reverting the worker-safety change.

Cost correction: with P players, A actor entities, B bodies, N all FormId entries, D candidates for one duplicate form, E hook events, U component changes and Q queued classifications, unique ownership lookups are expected O(E), duplicates O(E*D), updates O(U*(1+D)), startup O(N), storage O(N+A), plus unmeasured mutex wait. No separate B scan or full per-tick actor scan was introduced. At most four classifications run per main frame, each with existing activation traversal limits but also script/alias visitor and lock costs. Q is now bounded by currently unclassified held contacts, not by a fixed number. A finite burst needs ceil(Q/4) frames: one distinct trigger/player is two frames at P=5 and three at P=10; 100/player is 125/250 frames. Sustained overload has no fixed latency bound. Retained TriggerGate player/contact/held/guard scans and release sorting are not O(1), and still lack a strict elapsed-time budget; occupancy membership comparisons can be quadratic in P per contact. Classification still allocates temporary traversal storage. No five- or ten-player performance signoff is implied.


## ov-b1 round 2: verify1 resolution (2026-10-02)

Read all of `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/conductor/ov-b1-verify1.log`. **Conclusion: its ITEMS-05 overclaim objection is correct. ITEMS-05, FRAME-28 and FRAME-24 remain partly fixed; the overall crash/thread-safety goal is OPEN.** The null-extension and split-authority-read defects are corrected; the map no longer throws on missing rank. This does not resolve the worker drain, all registry writer lanes, native lifetimes or main-End UI/CEF work. High confidence in source/static evidence; no changed plugin build, paired run, crash resolution or five/ten-player performance is claimed.

The complete point-by-point response, eight-item disposition, next-run observations and risks are in `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/overnight/report-b1.md`. Durable sources, fixtures, checks.log, hashes and native outputs live in `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/overnight/b1-round2-evidence/`. Reproduce with Python 3.12 and `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/overnight/b1-round2-checks.py`; it does not invoke xmake or write shared build outputs.

### Primary sources and adopted/rejected approaches

- Searched the web for CommonLib task dispatch and EnTT lifecycle implementations before editing. Read [CommonLibSSE-NG Interfaces.cpp](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/src/SKSE/Interfaces.cpp): AddTask forwards a delegate to the SKSE interface. Rejected treating that wrapper as proof that this mod's RunnerService drains on main; its own call chain and the executable determine that. Existing X.00 mailboxes remain the explicit phase choice.
- Read the installed EnTT v3.10.0 [registry.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/registry.hpp), [sparse_set.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/sparse_set.hpp), and [sigh_storage_mixin.hpp](https://github.com/skypjack/entt/blob/v3.10.0/src/entt/entity/sigh_storage_mixin.hpp). Public clear follows destruction notification; rejected the review's claim that normal clear silently bypasses this index. Typed/full-clear tests confirm it. Direct identity writes and pool sorting do bypass publication, positively reproduced in new negative tests. They remain unsupported and must use patch or coordinated republication. Code/client search found no pool sort/owning-group calls; the assignment/signal audit found current remote epoch updates inside patch. This is not a proof about every alias or concurrent writer.
- Retrieved and read exact [TiltedCore v0.2.9 TaskQueue.cpp](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedCore/blob/v0.2.9/Code/core/src/TaskQueue.cpp), using raw HTTP when the browsing fetch failed; retained a local copy. Add is mutex protected; Drain retains the mutex across callbacks. Reentrant Add and unbounded wait remain risks. Rejected claiming producer locking makes callback engine/registry use safe.
- Read local [CommonLibSSE-NG UI.h](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/include/RE/U/UI.h): numItemMenus +0x164, numDisablePauseMenu +0x168. Retained the two distinct preconditions; native 82076 confirms the latter.

### Corrections and validation

Production changes this round: EquipManager.cpp's six hooks now check the extension and take one remote snapshot for suppression/local event selection; a missing extension keeps the native call but emits no replication event. Existing override and payload filters remain. Utils.cpp::OwnershipTokenIndex::Find checks all candidate ranks and returns no token for incomplete publication. It preserves valid duplicate first-match selection and zero-epoch rejection. No per-frame scan/probe was added.

The scale harness now compares both changed ownership helpers against compiled 25fa030d bodies instead of demanding text identity; other baseline assertions remain. A missing test-only IsDisabled accessor used by current Discovery diagnostic text was added so that suite compiles. No production Discovery behavior changed.

Fresh checks: four production MSVC /Zs passes (Utils, ActorExtension, EquipManager, InventoryService), stable file hashes; 20,000 atomic update rounds; 680,000 randomized ownership comparisons plus lifecycle edges; five/ten reader concurrency smoke checks with one registry writer; 167 actual-hook cases with native stubs; trigger main-frame scheduling cases and 52 assertions/seven existing trigger cases; integrated scale suite including baseline ownership comparisons; git diff --check. The missing-rank fault test changes only test friendship, not production Find. Unsupported direct write/sort cases are deliberately asserted stale, not claimed fixed. This is isolated validation, not a linked plugin, paired protocol run or player-count benchmark.

Corrected retained cost: E drained inventory/equip events scan N FormId entries, O(E*N) per client; incoming V messages add conservative O(V*(N+A)) lookup work, and the 500 ms weapon-state update visits A local actor candidates, before native/serialization/guard costs. Hook lookup itself is expected O(1) for unique keys or O(D) duplicate candidates plus unbounded mutex wait; publication costs O(U*(1+D)), storage O(N+A). Bodies B are included in actor counts, with no new separate body scan. Across P clients drain cost is sum(E_i*N_i). Trigger count budget remains four, with ceil(Q/4) burst frames and no fixed live backlog cap; retained OnUpdate bound remains O(A + I*P^2 + (H+G)*P + R log R) plus native calls. No strict end-to-end frame budget or five/ten-player measurements exist.

No source-only result proves the unreproduced door crash or other historical capture fixed. The remaining worker Calendar/LipSync/script-animation/inventory paths, main-End UI reads, CEF blocking locks, TaskQueue reentrancy and registry writer/lifetime contract require coordinated changes and coordinator-built paired validation. COMMON.md waives Reviewer A/B for this assignment; no fresh independent review is claimed.

## b2-dataloss source review (2026-10-02)

The seven ranked sections in `SkyrimAtlas/for-coop/MOD_FIXES.md` were rechecked against this working tree. Existing 2026-10-01 lobby, checkpoint, party and diagnostic edits were retained. No xmake build, linked plugin or paired in-game test was run; the outcomes below are source changes awaiting those checks.

Primary open-source code inspected before editing:

- [CommonLibSSE-NG TESForm.h](https://github.com/CharmedBaryon/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/main/include/RE/T/TESForm.h) declares virtual `AddChange` at slot 0x0A and `kTemporary` at bit 14. Adopted the slot/flag interpretation, then checked the actual 1.7.104 executable because CommonLib alone does not prove this version's change-map behavior.
- [SKSE64 Hooks_SaveLoad.cpp](https://github.com/ianpatt/skse64/blob/master/skse64/Hooks_SaveLoad.cpp) forwards save/load to native hooks and dispatches the load notifications around the native call. Adopted its separation of a queued request from native load execution; rejected using a Load_Impl hook to prevent the earlier queued death task.
- [TiltedEvolution CharacterService.cpp](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/TiltedEvolution/blob/dev/Code/client/Services/Generic/CharacterService.cpp) was inspected as the upstream multiplayer implementation. It does not provide an applicable pre-delete leveled-roll interception for the current mod's owner-pick protocol, so no upstream hook was copied.

Native code was queried with `sk.py fn id:<ID>` (the data singleton 403330 / 0x143223E18 is absent from the function corpus and was checked through its existing use in `Games/Forms.cpp`). Relevant findings:

| Item | 1.7.104 executable evidence and decision |
|---|---|
| FRAME-09 | ForceActorValue **0x1406C51F0 / 38467** -> **0x1406C5220 / 38468** does not publish a save change. Actor::SaveGame **0x140699230 / 37649** consumes change data. Replicated NPC value paths now queue `MarkChanged(0x2)` by form ID for X.00; player proxies are excluded. The bit is coalesced per changed form, with no actor scan. |
| QUEST-05 | ForceRefIntoAlias **0x1403D4FA0 / 25052** does not add the quest runtime bit; FillAliases **0x1403D3D10 / 25048** calls slot +0x50 with `0x10000000`, and shutdown **0x1403D44B0 / 25049** removes it. A successfully changed alias batch now queues that bit once; a partly applied batch queues it before returning failure. This is also the alias half of FRAME-09. |
| FRAME-07 | AddChange gate **0x140616CE0 / 35576** rejects form flag `0x4000`; save loop **0x140618260 / 35599** does not test the flag. Erase **0x140616EE0 / 35583** removes the current change-map entry (not an unloaded buffer). `Actor::Create` now accepts the caller's remote-player identity and only those proxies get bit 14 and immediate erase. NPC replicas retain their change forms. |
| FRAME-12 | Death timer **0x1407600F0 / 40748** calls task queue **0x140625850 / 35769** with `0xD0000100`. The new queue hook drops this task only while connected; X.00 uses `RespawnPlayerAt` and sends the existing `PlayerRespawnRequest` for an actually dead local player in an attached cell. The task hook preserves all four native arguments for other task kinds. |
| FRAME-36 | MoveTo_Impl **0x140A5D370 / 56626** enters should-move processing, and UpdateProcessLevel **0x140688740 / 37501** can dispose a non-persistent actor in an unloaded parent cell; GarbageCollector::Add is **0x14064DAB0 / 36459**. Interior moves now require an attached target, otherwise the form-ID move is retried through a 32-entry X.00 budget. Deleted-reference recreation and the old-cell-detaches-before-target-attaches case remain open. |
| ACTOR-23 | Roll **0x1401DCBA0 / 14374** calls list resolver **0x1401E9530 / 14752** and invokes `SetDelete(true)` at vtable +0x118 for empty/non-NPC results. `ProcessLeveledConforms` runs after this and discards deleted actors. A post-roll flag clear would race disposal and cannot safely reconstruct the temporary base. No hook was added without a pre-delete ownership/pick publication contract. This item remains open. |
| FRAME-08 | RemoveChange **0x140613900 / 35511** may retain a flag-zero entry for a 0xFF reference; erase **0x140616EE0 / 35583** removes a live entry. Non-owner drops now set bit 14 and queue an erase at X.00, where the flag is checked again. |

Cost: with P players, A tracked actors, B bodies, and C changed form IDs, the new save publication/erase path takes expected O(C) time and space per client between frames, with no full A or B scan and no P-squared work. Interior move retry handles at most 32 changed actors per main frame and stores at most one pending move per actor, O(A) worst-case storage; insertion/coalescing still scans pending moves, O(A) per new request. An unattached target can remain pending indefinitely and is checked once per queue rotation (up to 32 per frame). The death queue hook is O(1) per native request. Five-player throughput and timings are unmeasured.

COMMON.md explicitly waives Reviewer A and Reviewer B for this assignment because Reviewer B cannot start in this sandbox. Neither assistant reviewed this batch. The coordinator's changed build and two-PC observations are required before claiming in-game data-loss fixes.

Validation in this batch: MSVC `/Zs` passed for Actor.cpp, ActorValueService.cpp, QuestService.cpp, CheckpointSaves.cpp, CharacterService.cpp and SharedDropService.cpp using the cached client compile flags; Actor.cpp, ActorValueService.cpp, CheckpointSaves.cpp and CharacterService.cpp passed again after the final source corrections. `git -c core.safecrlf=false diff --check` passed. The network protocol was not changed. Save records, alias restoration, death routing, interior attach, and leveled actor behavior still require a coordinator build and paired observations.

## b3-quests source research (2026-10-02)

The ranked `SkyrimAtlas/for-coop/MOD_FIXES.md` entries QUEST-06, QUEST-11, QUEST-07, QUEST-12, QUEST-01, QUEST-03, QUEST-04, QUEST-24, QUEST-27, FRAME-30, FRAME-20, FRAME-18 and FRAME-34 were checked against the current worktree. The 2026-10-01 lobby, checkpoint, party and diagnostic changes were retained. No xmake build or two-PC session ran in this batch; the code changes below are source-level candidates, not observed in-game fixes.

Primary open-source code inspected before editing: [CommonLibSSE TESQuest.h](https://github.com/libxse/commonlibsse/blob/main/include/RE/T/TESQuest.h) defines quest enabled and repeat-stage flags, startup/shutdown stage flags, `EnsureQuestStarted`, and the runtime aliases and stage list. Adopted these layout/flag meanings, then checked the exact 1.7.104 native functions. Rejected directly starting follower quests because that also runs their startup fragments and promotes aliases again. The upstream [CommonLibSSE-NG TESObjectREFR.cpp](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/src/RE/T/TESObjectREFR.cpp) was inspected for reference/animation behavior; it has no multiplayer item mask or per-script control-lock ownership model to copy. The repository's existing `LeveledNpcSystem::GetOriginalBase` and `CharacterService::ApplyLeveledNpcPick` were inspected; the latter is owner-pick-driven, so an alias fill alone cannot safely request a host appearance resync.

| Item | SkyrimSE.exe 1.7.104 VA / Address Library ID and decision |
|---|---|
| QUEST-06 | `BGSRefAlias::Attach` **0x14039FDE0 / 24011**, force **0x1403D4FA0 / 25052**, promote **0x1403D9200 / 25083**. Deferring the pre-start force avoids an attach before native promotion; startup hook remains. |
| QUEST-11 | Papyrus optional guard **0x140A1B5D0 / 55286**, direct clear **0x1403D4BC0 / 25051**. Require two distinct leader snapshots before clearing a required alias; the host sends a second snapshot despite identical-fill dedupe. Optional clears remain immediate. |
| QUEST-07 | `SetStage` **0x1403D0DC0 / 25004** checks the enabled bit. Keep a disabled follower's alias/stage packet in the existing bounded queue, and judge an applied itemless stage by its done bit. Normal leader sends are bundled; direct legacy `NotifyQuestUpdate` is not queued. Follower quest auto-start was rejected because QUEST-02's fragment suppression and QUEST-06's promotion ordering are unresolved. Host-only quests that never start locally can fill the original 256-message global cap; its preexisting sticky overflow safety then stops further quest-stage application until session restart. This remains partial. |
| QUEST-12 | Attach **0x14039FDE0 / 24011** calls reset **0x140678A50 / 37323** for an unloaded leveled actor. The native loaded-cell predicate **0x1401A2200 / 13308** and `Actor::IsLeveled` **0x140301630 / 20229** were read and used. Force is deferred until the cell loads; the existing three-second hold can still let a stage run without that alias. |
| QUEST-01/03/04 | Runner **0x1403E19B0 / 25252** marks a stage done and calls item runner **0x1403E1F60 / 25260**; its failed-item branch posts through **0x1403E2720 / 25272**. `SetStage` **0x1403D0DC0 / 25004** can return false for an itemless stage even when it was marked done. Only that follower success test changed. The one-update-per-runner hook, host item mask, follower item hook and protocol propagation remain coupled work; repeat-stage guards must stay until that work is complete. |
| QUEST-24 | Native contact enter **0x140407110 / 26041**, repeat **0x140406D80 / 26038**. Recover the first repeat as enter for the local player too; track delivered non-party contacts so a swallowed enter cannot be followed by a delivered leave. Loading/authority loss clears contacts; a 4096-pair cap bounds long-lived unloaded references. Party-held delivery remains controlled by the existing occupancy/held path. |
| QUEST-27/FRAME-30 | VM latent return **0x1414B4840 / 104807**, DisableNoWait **0x140A45540 / 56156**, EnableNoWait **0x140A457B0 / 56160**, nonlatent PlayAnimation **0x140A47370 / 56205** and PlayAnimationAndWait **0x140A473F0 / 56206**. Switched live replay/disable/enable to nonlatent calls and Delete/Reset and the remaining wait wrapper to an explicit sentinel stack ID. Both animation natives reject Actor references, a preexisting replay limit. Sentinel treatment by the VM remains inferred and needs the requested wait-stack test. |
| FRAME-20/18 | Papyrus control lock/unlock **0x140A23EE0 / 55454**, **0x140A23FC0 / 55455**; load restoration **0x14074E600 / 40540**; Toggle/Store/Load **0x140CEFAA0 / 68545**, **0x140CEFB30 / 68546**, **0x140CEFB50 / 68547**. Camera release now updates the saved snapshot. A per-script ledger and load-scoped orphan release remain open; a blanket host-mask copy was rejected because it can release legitimate local owners. |
| FRAME-34 | `QuitToMainMenu` **0x140A253E0 / 55490** starts the fade; callback **0x140A2E9A0 / 55646** sets `Main+0x11`; Fader process **0x14092BE70 / 51523** and fade-complete **0x14092C020 / 51526** expose active/callback state. The fallback now waits `fLoadGameFadeSecs:General` + 2 s and requires a closed Main Menu and no active fade callback. The preexisting `CheckpointSaves::IsPending()` gate was retained. |

Cost: with P players, A tracked actors and B bodies, these edits add no full P, A or B per-frame scan. Quest alias retry retains its 16-quest-per-frame sweep and 16-message retry budget; each snapshot comparison is O(alias count log alias count) and per-quest alias storage remains bounded by the existing 4096-quest/256-message caps. The new host required-empty count and follower clear check are O(alias count) per snapshot. Trigger contact storage is O(min(current non-party contact pairs, 4096)) plus the existing party occupancy, with expected O(1) mutation per native contact; it is cleared on leave, load and authority loss. Papyrus and camera changes are O(1) per call. These are design costs, not measured five-player throughput.

Reviewer A and Reviewer B independently inspected the source and native evidence. Both found QUEST-07's unbounded waiting problem and QUEST-12's three-second stage/alias gap; these are reported as partial. Reviewer B found that the first TriggerGate draft retained stale contacts while the host was inactive; the contact ledgers now clear when world-script delivery is suspended. Reviewer A found the first QUEST-12 predicate did not match native Attach; the code now calls the cited `Actor::IsLeveled` ID 20229. Both checked the second required-empty snapshot flow. They agreed that Actor `PlayAnimation` and `PlayAnimationAndWait` both reject Actor refs, so actor replay remains a preexisting gap. Reviewer A suggested a 16-stage per-quest queue cap, but it was withdrawn: it would trigger the pre-`25fa030d` sticky overflow earlier than the original 256-packet safety cap. This resolves the review suggestion against the source baseline while leaving QUEST-07 partial. No reviewer agreement is treated as paired validation.

## b4-actors source research (2026-10-02)

The ranked `SkyrimAtlas/for-coop/MOD_FIXES.md` entries ACTOR-01, ACTOR-10, ACTOR-02, ACTOR-06/FRAME-37, ACTOR-09, ACTOR-05, ACTOR-18/19, and QUEST-19/20/22/23 were checked against this worktree. The 2026-10-01 lobby, checkpoint, party and diagnostic changes were retained. Source-level changes require the coordinator's build and paired observation before an in-game fix can be claimed.

Primary open-source code inspected: [CommonLibSSE-NG TESObjectREFR.cpp](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/src/RE/T/TESObjectREFR.cpp) shows `ActivateRef` using SE relocation 19796 and retained reference-handle lookup; adopted the native activation boundary and handle lifetime, with exact 1.7.104 behavior verified below. [CommonLibSSE TESObjectREFR.h](https://github.com/Ryan-rsm-McKenzie/CommonLibSSE/blob/master/include/RE/T/TESObjectREFR.h) declares `SetActivationBlocked`, open-state flags and door/reference interfaces; adopted the reference-property model, but rejected copying its version-specific offsets without executable confirmation. [CommonLibSSE Actor.cpp](https://github.com/libxse/commonlibsse/blob/main/src/RE/A/Actor.cpp) was inspected for a reusable combat/bleedout authority policy; none exists, so no code was copied. The current mod's upstream multiplayer paths and `SkyrimAtlas` source evidence were preferred for ownership rules.

| Item | 1.7.104 executable evidence checked with `sk.py fn <VA>` and decision |
|---|---|
| ACTOR-01 | ActorProcess **0x14067C380 / 37356** is the locomotion hook; AI tick **0x140679D50 / 37347** is separate. Corrected the comment. A broad AI gate was rejected because it also controls scene/timer behavior and runs on workers. |
| ACTOR-10 | KnockExplosion **0x1407237C0 / 39895** can fail its 37485/vfunc 0xC5 gates or queue work. The copy now verifies knock bits on a later X.00 pass, retries at 250 ms up to eight times, and retains the graph fallback. The log identifies both possible native gates, not a measured individual failure. |
| ACTOR-02 | Pending-door runner **0x1406F6E40 / 39408** reaches ActivateRef **19796**; setter **0x1406F6E10 / 39407** uses a handle pointer. Remote NPC activation is blocked at the hook, and its pending handle/+0x470 are cleared. Remote players remain allowed. |
| ACTOR-06/FRAME-37 | MoveToMiddleHigh **0x140696060 / 37624** revives state 7 during process transitions; MoveToMiddleLow **37623** and MoveToLow **37622** have related stores. `ApplyPeers` now restores down state when unchanged replicated data meets a locally revived actor. |
| ACTOR-09 | Knock machine **0x140723B60 / 39896** and StartGetUp **0x140735C20 / 40150** run independently of movement suppression. The owner knock bits are now serialized and relayed. The get-up hook, blocked-attempt tracking and follow teardown remain open because calling 40150 while the native task is also pending can play get-up twice or leave the controlled driver bound. |
| ACTOR-05 | ForceCurrent **0x1406C51F0 / 38467** reaches HandleHealthDamage **0x1406CC3D0 / 38606**, then NPC bleedout **0x1406CEF50 / 38631**. The latter now declines for remote NPCs. Host-driven NPC down/up state and timer replication remain open; the existing remote KillImpl refusal was preserved. |

Start requests from update/notify handlers are deduplicated and drained on X.00 with a 32-pair frame budget and per-pair deduplication. Papyrus StopCombat **0x140A03480 / 54770** can asynchronously destroy a newly started controller; unused `StartCombatEx` was deleted. |
| QUEST-19/20 | DOOR::Activate **0x140283210 / 17922** can request actor fade/travel; fade reset **0x1406FE050 / 39453** and pending-door setter **0x1406F6E10 / 39407** were inspected. Load-door replay using a remote copy is dropped. ACTOR-02 prevents new replica pending-door activation. A full event-driven X.00 fade-state cleanup for already pending state 4 remains open. |
| QUEST-22 | Papyrus BlockActivation **0x140A450A0 / 56148** calls ExtraDataList::SetExtraFlags **0x1401726C0 / 12042** with bit 1. A world-state value now carries the host decision; the follower applies it on the main thread. Host baseline sampling uses the existing 32-reference budget. |

Observe an immediate bit flip even on false return. Before applying an open state, defer to an active local opener and otherwise clear the stale handle. A queued setter still needs next-frame confirmation in a paired test. |

Cost with P players, A tracked actors and B bodies: activation/open/bleedout hooks are O(1) per native call; revive checks are O(P) in the existing peer loop; ragdoll retries are O(1) per changed stream within the existing stream budget, with no new B scan. Combat queue insertion is expected O(1), storage O(P*A) unique actor-target pairs, and drain at most 32 pairs per X.00 frame; existing candidate selection remains O(P*A) and can include O(P^2*A) opponent checks in its preexisting loop. BlockActivation baseline uses the existing 32-reference per-frame sweep, O(1) per sampled reference. Five-player latency and throughput are unmeasured.

The local Reviewer A and Reviewer B assistants were not callable from this sandbox; neither reviewed this batch. The COMMON.md task note explicitly records that limitation. Isolated corpse protocol tests passed (3449 assertions); isolated world-state tests passed (1468 assertions). MSVC `/Zs` passed for Actor, CombatService, StealthService, CorpseRagdollService, ReviveService, ObjectService, WorldStateService, TESObjectREFR and World. No xmake build or paired game test ran.

## b5-items inventory, XP and magic review (2026-10-02)

Primary source search and code inspection: [CommonLibSSE-NG ActorEquipManager.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/A/ActorEquipManager.h) declares exact-list equip and unequip parameters, which supports reacquiring a live ExtraDataList after inventory insertion. [CommonLibSSE-NG MagicTarget.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/M/MagicTarget.h) declares VisitEffects and effect-specific DispelEffect; this is why a whole-spell RemoveSpell relay was rejected for ITEMS-13. [CommonLibSSE-NG Actor.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/A/Actor.h) exposes ForEachPerk, HasPerkEntries and ForEachPerkEntry, supporting a future full perk overlay, but the current detection-only snapshot is insufficient. The local `C:/Tools/ref/TiltedEvolution-rwf/Code/client/Services/Generic/InventoryService.cpp` uses base-form-only equip, which was rejected for stack-sensitive replicas. [SkyMP's game-thread issue](https://github.com/skyrim-multiplayer/skymp/issues/653) independently reports native calls from non-game threads failing; native reconciliation here remains in the existing main-thread guard. Exact runtime semantics and IDs below come from the 1.7.104 executable corpus, not those headers.

| Item | Native evidence rechecked with `sk.py fn` | Decision and remaining limit |
|---|---|---|
| ITEMS-04 | worker **0x1406DE920 / 38929**, apply equip **0x1406B2570 / 38001**, apply unequip **0x1406B2E50 / 38002** | Moved item notifications from worker to apply hooks, including immediate queued calls; conflict unequips remain suppressed. No paired run yet. |
| ITEMS-09 | AddItem **0x14022C310 / 16053**, AddWorldReference **0x14022B990 / 16052**, RemoveItem **0x14022DA70 / 16059** | Deferred. A funnel hook needs owner lookup, worker-to-main event handoff, outer-hook deduplication, load suppression and a distinct soul-gem mutation path. Adding one funnel without those would double inventory deltas. |
| ITEMS-18 | HasEntries **0x14069F990 / 37701**, VisitEntries **0x14069F9C0 / 37702**, HasPerk **0x14069F920 / 37698**, player ForEachPerk **0x14075BB00 / 40680** | Deferred. All 92 entry points need a full ordered perk/rank protocol and an immutable, job-thread-safe per-replica publication. Native perk grants were rejected because they would change gameplay state twice. |

Repeated identical deltas still add inventory count according to the preexisting protocol; worn selection itself checks before equipping. |

The 40489 book/trainer/console path remains intentionally outside the combat-XP sharing set pending a separate product decision. |

Receiver's existing scoped factor override keeps the factor at one while native entry 22 runs once. |

Replay sets/restores the shooter's high-process dual flag around Launch and sets projectile bit 28 when needed. Concentration replay sets that flag until the remote interrupt. |

Scale with P players, A tracked actors and B bodies: no new P, A or B full frame scan was added. Equip, inventory and magic work is event-driven, O(1) in P/A/B per event plus the affected actor's inventory or active-effect list. XP sends scan a fixed 24 skill slots once per second, O(1) in P/A/B. The new worn dirty queue holds at most 64 form IDs and processes at most eight per main frame; lookup is O(8A) only on dirty frames, while the existing eight-target cursor and 250 ms target census remain. Memory is O(min(A,64)) for dirty IDs. Network adds one skill varint per XP packet, one dual bit per projectile packet, and one Inventory::Entry only on unambiguous equipped-stack notifications. Five-player CPU and bandwidth remain unmeasured.

Review and validation: the local Reviewer A and Reviewer B assistants were unavailable in this sandbox per `C:/Tools/skyrim_re/agent/COMMON.md`; neither reviewed this batch. Syntax checks used cached MSVC flags with `/Zs` and no xmake build. The isolated `Code/tests/items_protocol.cpp` run passed 20 assertions in three cases against the newly compiled serializers and message factories; it does not establish paired in-game behavior. No two-PC observation of this changed build exists, so in-game fixes are not claimed.

## b6-world crime, factions, time and weather (2026-10-02)

The requested `SkyrimAtlas/for-coop/MOD_FIXES.md` entries were rechecked in the specified order against this worktree, including the October 1 changes. This batch did not run xmake or an in-game session. "Fixed" below means the source change is present, not that the in-game symptom has been observed to disappear.

Primary open-source source inspected before editing: [CommonLibSSE-NG Actor.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/A/Actor.h) declares the vtable slot 0xB8 crime-gold query and the six-argument StealAlarm and three-argument TrespassAlarm signatures. Those signatures were adopted only after checking the 1.7.104 functions; its relocations were not copied. [CommonLibSSE-NG Actor.cpp](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/src/RE/A/Actor.cpp) shows direct alarm wrappers, and [GameSettingCollection.cpp](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/src/RE/G/GameSettingCollection.cpp) shows a settings lookup. The latter was rejected for this batch because its singleton relocation is not among the cited addresses. The local [TiltedEvolution-rwf source](https://github.com/rfortier/TiltedEvolution-rwf) was inspected at `Code/client/Games/Skyrim/Actor.cpp`, `PlayerCharacter.cpp`, `Services/Generic/CalendarService.cpp` and `WeatherService.cpp`: it confirms the inherited remove-all faction, unconditional fine, independently integrated clock and force-weather paths. Those behaviors were rejected where native evidence shows side effects or overwritten fields. The [Skyrim Together feature notes](https://github.com/tiltedphoques/gitbook-wiki/blob/main/general-information/features.md) describe leader weather and server time, but do not supply these native contracts. No open-source witness-to-host crime relay or jail tick ending protocol was found that could safely be copied.

| Item | Native evidence checked with `sk.py fn <VA>` and source decision |
|---|---|
| ITEMS-32 | Charge **0x14069D730 / 37679** resolves the criminal handle at +0x0C and charges only the local player; StartCombat **0x1406C93B0 / 38561** handles native AI bookkeeping. Deferred: `StealthService` has only the damage Threat path. A safe crime packet still needs witness identity, an ID +0x48 dedupe, a worker-to-main copy, server validation and a bounded host guard response. Sending a raw Crime pointer or selecting arbitrary guards was rejected. |
| ITEMS-33 | PayFine **0x14075A660 / 40659** calls slot +0x5C0, corresponding to GetCrimeGoldValue **0x1407591A0 / 40641**, then story/gold/clear-jail/combat side effects. Cleanup **0x140782900 / 41363** runs in the frame work. `PayCrimeGoldToAllFactions` now queues once and pays only factions whose query is nonzero in the X.00 main-loop window. |
| ITEMS-30 | StealAlarm **0x140681DE0 / 37422** and TrespassAlarm **0x140683C70 / 37427** can swap a teammate criminal to the host player. Both entry hooks now return only for a host-side remote-player puppet; replayed activation and teammate behavior remain. |

Deferred: `NotifyDeathStateChange` and health deltas have no killer, and `Actor::Kill` supplies null. The owner must preserve a validated lethal attacker ID through damage, death and server relay before a once-only main-thread murder call can be safe. |

The leader path is unchanged. X.00 now maintains +0x60, +0x50 and +0x1B4 and leaves +0x1B8 to the engine. The default fWeatherTransMin/Max values 0.01/0.25 are used; a game-setting override can change the transition length and still needs verification or a supported setting lookup. |
| FRAME-03 | SetParentCell **0x140747D60 / 40466** calls SetClimate **0x140414F00 / 26239**; Sky update **0x140411EC0 / 26229** skips weather in mode 1. The leader now withholds mode-1 packets; packets carry worldspace; the follower ignores other worldspaces and reasserts +0x60 after a climate reset without ForceWeather. |

Cost with P players, A tracked actors and B bodies: alarm and save hooks, calendar and weather X.00 work, and weather packets add O(1) CPU and state per call/frame, independent of A and B. The fine queue is one atomic flag and at most ten fixed faction queries per requested payment. The faction diff compares entries within one changed actor's faction lists, O(F squared) for that actor's F factions on a received update; it adds no per-frame actor or body scan. The existing owner faction census still scans tracked A every two seconds; this batch did not expand it. Network additions are one GameId plus one bit per faction payload, one float per time model and one GameId per sky packet; weather remains at most 2 Hz during blending. Five-player cost and paired correctness are not measured.

Validation: MSVC `/Zs` passed for the edited client, server, encoding and common translation units using cached flags, with no xmake build. An isolated binary linked the changed production serializers and passed 14 assertions in two cases, including faction crime fields, DaysPassed round trip and midnight advance, and sky worldspace round trips. `git -c core.safecrlf=false diff --check` was used. A new build must be exercised on two PCs before any in-game issue is called fixed. COMMON.md waives local Reviewer A/B for this assignment because Reviewer B cannot start in this sandbox; neither independently reviewed this batch.

## b7-playthrough small correctness and time skip (2026-10-02)

Primary source search and source inspection preceded these edits. [CommonLibSSE-NG PlayerControlsData.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/P/PlayerControlsData.h) names +0x4F as the held-state setup byte, consistent with native ProcessEvent; its +0x4E field is unnamed, so the release meaning comes from the executable. [LoadingMenu.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/L/LoadingMenu.h) declares +0x58 as a TESLoadScreen array and +0x78 as the chosen pointer; the executable shows the array shrinks after selection. [TESTopicInfo.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/T/TESTopicInfo.h) declares `saidOnce` at +0x3A, ENAM Info Refusal bit 4 and change flag 0x80000000; [TESTopicInfoEvent.h](https://github.com/alandtse/CommonLibSSE-NG/blob/ng/include/RE/T/TESTopicInfoEvent.h) was inspected but not used because the native 25616 detour catches the exact local state transition without replaying fragments. The local reference clone is `C:/Tools/ref/CommonLibSSE-NG`; these declarations were checked against 1.7.104 before adopting them. The inherited [Skyrim Together Reborn source](https://github.com/AHilyard/SkyrimTogetherReborn) was inspected for the server calendar and subtitle path. Reusing SubtitleRequest was rejected: server CharacterService::OnSubtitleRequest drops TopicFormId and subtitles do not cover every posted INFO. Reusing SetTimeCommandRequest was rejected because it carries only hour/minute and loses the date and DaysPassed events.
